- past holidays stay days off for good (`holPast`, fix round 3, W1-8-01): the monthly refetch, another country and the loading or failed states keep the list's entries before today (and their unticks) beside the new list; "No public holidays" keeps them as `{country:'', list, skip, fetched:0}` (null only when none are past); Settings lists only the coming ones; before, a holiday that fell out of the refetched list (a month back) became a workday after the fact and was recorded as worn, "a day you didn't open the app"
- (fix round 2) `toggleDay`, `setDarkShirts` (and setup.js `setupSetDress`, `setupCancel`) save the settings before releasing the week's locks (`clearLocks`), as dress.js `setDress`: tapped then swiped away within ~100 ms, the change was lost
- (fix round 2) an empty city search clears the last results; a search in another language asks each result again by id in English (`cityEnglish`: Open-Meteo `/v1/get?id=&language=en`), so the stored name is English, with the name typed shown beside it on the button (`alt`, dropped by `setLocation`)
- the theme button's first tap goes to the theme the phone isn't showing, then the phone's own, then automatic, each with a toast ("Dark theme. Tap again for light."; fix round 3, W2-14-13: on a light phone the first tap chose Light and nothing changed)
- a city search says how many places it found, and picking one "Location set to …" (`announce`); the field keeps the keyboard after a pick (W2-19-01)

### `start.js`

- **the router is nav.js** (the restructure S3f, 2026-11-26; the owner's choice of 2026-11-25): `show`, `pageIn`, `subOpen`, `subBack`, `openAdd`, `pagesOn` (a getter; start.js sets it with `setPagesOn`, and a failed start-up drops the page asked for with `setPageWant(null)`), `showWanted` and `pageBack` moved there unchanged; start.js keeps the start-up chain, `startPlain`, `onSyncedData`, the handlers (`tabTap`, `wardAddTap`, `addBackTap`, `reloadTap`) and the `backOn('sub'/'set', pageBack)` pins
- start.js pins `pageBack` (nav.js) at load above its own declarations, as it pinned its own before: without back.js or nav.js that line throws, start.js stops there (`startUp` then meets its `let`/`const` before they are set) and the start-up card shows, as main showed for back.js (measured: with the pins in nav.js, back.js missing drew "Today couldn’t be shown" and nav.js missing drew Today)
- nav.js is a required file (`tests/load-contract.json`; without it start-up can't show Today, so the start-up card shows) in the ui kit, right before add.js; it imports only bus.js
- the tabs' and Add's handlers stay in start.js on purpose: a tab tapped while the scripts load counts (is shown once Today is up) only from start.js's place in the list, as before (`tests/tabload.test.js`, `tests/modload.test.js`); with them in nav.js a tap made while add.js … theme.js load would be shown too, and with start.js last in the list a tap made while safety.js … about.js load would be dropped
- nav.js reaches Settings through bus.js (settings.js pins them): `page-showing` (Settings closes, where `show` called `closeSettings()`), `page-back` (Back with no page over Settings: `saveSettings` when open), `sub-opening` (Settings fills in its open categories and scroll) and `sub-back` (Settings opens as it was); the gear tapped during loading is opened by start.js after `showWanted()`
- nav.js asks the setup, What to buy and the packing list through sockets (bus.js `ask`): `setup-yield` (setup.js `setupYield`), `shop-close` (shop.js `closeShop`, when another page is shown), `pack-show` (pack.js `packShow`), where the `typeof f==='function'` guards were
- nav.js still reads the pages it draws and the item sheet as globals (`renderToday`, `renderWardrobe`, `renderAdd`, `statsShow`/`renderStats`, `npsLeave`, `setAddFor`, `editing`, `dropItem`, `renderItem`, `noMotion`): forward reads left for the Add, item sheet and Today PRs (S3c-S3e), which can pin or serve their part
- an ES module since 2026-11-24, its code unchanged, importing nothing; its bridge is its first statement (nav.js's is above `backOn('sub', …)`, its first top-level call that can throw); index.html's last script reads `startUp` from it as a global, and the head's catcher checks `window.startUp`; nav.js's `pagesOn` is a getter; `tests/actsindex.test.js` watches `show`, `openAdd` and `subBack` at their handlers (`VIA`: `tabTap` with the control's data-page, `wardAddTap`, `addBackTap`)
- core.js … itemsheet.js lost 11 guards that are always true (C2-10, 2026-11-22): three on an earlier script's name (detect.js `isOcp` and `CUSTOM_PAT`, itemsheet.js `isFavItem`) and eight on a later script's name that nothing loaded before that script can reach (app.js `toast` twice and `settingsOpen`, add.js `isConnected` in `refineAgain` / `patternAgain`, item-photo.js `remakeLeft` / `remakeSpend`, item-view.js `layerOpen`)
- that range keeps 61 guards on later scripts' names in app.js … itemsheet.js: a tap, a file picked or the start-up sync can reach them while later scripts are still loading (`show`, `renderAdd`, `renderItem`, `renderWardrobe`, `put`, `ask`, `save`); and those of core.js, planner.js and analysis.js (also loaded by analysis-worker.js and by Node tests on their own), draw.js and mannequin.js (the painter's) and the browser features (`ResizeObserver`, `AbortController`, `HTMLCanvasElement`, `reportError`, `window`)
- `show(t)` before start-up has drawn Today only notes the page (`pagesOn` false, `pageWant`; 2026-11-22): a tab tapped while later scripts were still loading threw (`renderWardrobe` needs wardrobe.js `wardKeep`, Insights stats.js `renderStats`) and start-up then went to Today whatever was tapped
- `startUp()` sets `pagesOn`, shows Today, starts its jobs and then shows the tab asked for (`showWanted`: the last one tapped, a tab in `TABS` other than Today); a failed start-up (`startFailed`) stays on Today, where its message is; `tests/tabload.test.js` (ui.js held back by the routes, Chromium and WebKit)
- a test that must tap or read the page while a script is held back: `waitForFunction` with `{polling: 50}` (the default polls by animation frame) and, on WebKit, a click sent from the page; Playwright's WebKit draws no frame and takes no input while the parser waits for a script

- moving between pages animates (`pageIn`, from `show`): the page arriving eases in 28px from its tab's side (`TABS` today, wardrobe, stats, left to right) with a fade, `PAGE_MS` 320 on `MONTH_EASE`; a sub-page (add, pack, care) rises 14px; the one leaving hides at once (nothing laid out twice); the tab marker is `nav::before` sliding by `--ti` (`.tab-none` hides it); reduced motion switches instantly; `tests/tabmove.test.js`
- `show(t)` ends with `packShow(t)` (pack.js), which hides or shows `#pack`
- leaving the Wardrobe tab empties `#wardrobe` (`show()`): its grid (about 4,900 elements at 107 items) is drawn again on coming back; `.gi` has `content-visibility:auto` (`contain-intrinsic-size:auto 230px`)
- the stats, packing and care pages' ‹ (`.pback`) calls `subBack()`: `openStats`/`openPack`/`openCare` call `subOpen()` first, which notes the tab under them, its scroll, and, when Settings was open, its open categories and scrollTop; back restores them (`openSettings()` alone folds everything and scrolls to the top); opened from Today it goes back to Today
- the phone's Back on those pages and in Settings goes back one level: Settings and those pages share ONE history entry (back.js's app entry, kind 'set' or 'sub'; `backPush`/`backDrop`); Back on it calls `pageBack` (`backOn('sub')`/`backOn('set')`): `subBack`, or closes Settings like Done (`saveSettings`); opening a page from Settings hands the entry on, its ‹ hands it back (`backHold`); left another way (the ‹, a tab, closing Settings) the entry is taken away with our own `history.back()`, let pass by the listener; `tests/back.test.js`
- the tabs have no Back entries (Back there leaves the app)
- the setup, the piece picker and What to buy take the same entry (new-user audit 2026-11-14: Back left the app from all three): kind 'setup' (`setupOpen`, again in `setupGo` after a tap; `setupClose` drops it), 'pick' (`openPicker`/`closePicker`), 'shop' (`openShop`/`closeShop`); Back on it runs what each registered (`backOn`: setup.js `setupBack`, history.js, shop.js): the setup steps back one step (on Welcome it stays open) and pushes again, the picker and What to buy close; `tests/back.test.js`
- an entry asked for while our own step back is on its way, or while the address holds `#invite=` (the entry would carry it and Back would land on it), waits in `backWant` and is made by `backWanted()` when the step back lands (the popstate) or `dropInviteHash` (account.js) runs: Add after the setup's last step, the setup after "Run setup again" in Settings, the setup opened at start-up from an invitation
- iOS may skip a history entry made without a tap; the setup's entry is pushed again from `setupGo` (a tap) for that, the start-up one can't be checked off the phone
- a layer over a page borrows the page's entry instead of making a second one (fix round 2, 2026-11-15): `backBorrow(kind)` gives it the entry (or makes its own when none is held, or takes the place of an entry still waiting in `backWant`) and returns whose it was; on closing every layer calls `backReturn(kind, was, fromBack)`: by a tap it hands the entry back (or drops the layer's own entry); closed by Back, the entry is gone, so it makes the page's again (`backPush(was)`)
- kinds 'ask' (a question, ui.js) and 'kit' (the tour, the close-up guide, the Home Screen sheet, the import sheet, kit.js) use it: Back closes the question as Cancel (`askBack`) and the sheet as ✕ (`kitClose(true)`; while an import is being written it stays and its entry is made again), and the page under them (Settings, Add, the item sheet) stays open with its entry; `tests/back.test.js`
- kind 'rd' (the reading sheet, colpick.js `rdOpen` → `backBorrow('rd')`, kept in `rdHist`): Back closes it as × (`rdBack` → `rdClose(true)`, a reading under way is aborted), the item sheet or Add under it stays with its entry; a reading run again in the open sheet (Try again, Retake) keeps the one entry (the Back audit, 2026-11-15: Back closed the page under it and left the sheet up)
- "Run setup again" on its first step: Back closes it as its Cancel (`setupCancel`); a first setup still stays open there (the Back audit, 2026-11-15)
- the Back audit (2026-11-15): every layer checked in Chromium and WebKit with touch (`tests/backgesture.test.js`): Back closes it alone, × leaves no entry (no dead press), repeated open/close keeps `history.length`; in-page states (Today's folds, the picture's zoom and open swatch, Settings' categories) have no entries, so Back there leaves the app as on any tab
- kind 'nps' (the "Add without a photo" sheet, add.js `npsOpen` → `backBorrow('nps')`): Back closes it as × (`npsBack`; a changed card asks first and its entry is made again), Add stays with its entry; `show()` leaving Add calls `npsLeave` first
- with the item sheet's entry and the app entry both on back.js's stack (a question or the close-up guide opened over the item sheet), Back takes the latest of the two only: the app entry's sheet closes and the item sheet keeps its entry; the next Back closes the item sheet
- the tab shown has `aria-current="page"` (`show`; W2-19-05)
- (CR-7-03) a start-up error after the stores were read keeps that wardrobe (`startFailed`; `startRead`, `startTook`), says in a toast that it is safe on this phone and nothing was deleted, notes the error (`errNote(e,'Start-up')`, sent by a tester's phone) and still runs `safetyStart`; only stores that can't be read leave it empty (`storageDown`)
- the stored-record check failing as a whole (`cleanStored`) puts each record through `cleanRecord`/`cleanDay` alone, leaving out only the ones that fail
- Today that can't be drawn at start-up is a plain card (`startPlain`): "Today couldn't be shown", the wardrobe is safe, Try again (a reload)
- `show()` drops a 'sub' entry again after `closeSettings()`, since Settings over Add hands Add's entry back as it closes (CR-7-04)
- `tests/shellfix.test.js`: the shell's code-review fixes (CR-7-01, 03, 04, 05, 06) as a person meets them

### `ui.js`

- shared interface pieces (after app.js; nothing runs at load)
- `connNote` (unused) is gone (CR-7-14)
- `askConfirm(message, {ok, cancel, danger})` → `Promise<boolean>`: a bottom sheet (`#ask-sheet`/`#ask-backdrop`) above everything; first paragraph = question; Cancel, Escape, outside tap or a second question → false (`askAnswer`); `danger` = red button, focus on Cancel
- `tell(message)`: a toast up 4–9 s by length
- the close-up chooser's mode 'read' (`CU_COPY.read`, colpick.js `rdStart`): "Close-up for Claude" / "Claude reads the color and the weave from it. …", with the paper line
- the close-up chooser (2026-11-15, the owner's variant A from `C:\code\audit-2026-11-13\custom-colour-texture\closeup-chooser-mockups\`): `closeupBtn({label, cls, mode:'add'|'replace'|'retake', act, remove, cloth})` (`act`: the caller's file handler attributes, `remove`: its Remove handler's name; see `act.js`) gives a close-up button's HTML; on a touch screen (`cuTouch()`: `pointer: coarse`) a plain button calling `closeupPick`, without touch today's label around a file input (no sheet)
- `closeupPick` opens `#cu-sheet`/`#cu-backdrop` (the questions' style, z-index 2010/2011: under a question and over the colour sheet): the cloth-and-paper drawing (52 px `.wbpic`), the title and line (`CU_COPY`: "Fabric close-up" / "Replace close-up" / "Retake close-up"), Take close-up (`#cu-take`, `capture="environment"`), the paper reminder (not on Retake), Choose from photos (`#cu-lib`, no capture), Remove close-up (`#cu-rm`, quiet red, only with `remove`) and Cancel
- its two choices are labels around their file inputs, so the person's own tap opens the camera or photos (iPhone blocks a click from code); a chosen file closes the sheet and goes to the button's handler (`addTexture`, `replaceTexture`, `cnRetake`)
- Cancel, a tap outside (not in the first `ASK_SETTLE` ms), Escape (a capturing window listener ahead of the colour sheet's) and the phone's Back (`backBorrow('cu')`, `backOn('cu')` → `closeupBack`; the colour sheet's `backOver` stands aside while the latest entry is 'cu') close it with nothing changed; Tab stays inside; it is in `LAYER_Q`, so the page under it is inert; `tests/closeupsheet.test.js` (Chromium and WebKit, touch and no touch)
- no `confirm()`/`alert()`/`prompt()` in the app (`tests/ui.test.js` checks); tests click `#ask-ok` / `#ask-cancel`
- `isConnected()`; `NEED_CONN` is the one "needs a connection" sentence
- `needConn(btn)` hides a server-only button's row (`.connhide`) while not connected; server-only weave reading (`claudeOnly`) is hidden too
- `.conn-only` sections hide under `body.nocon`, with a `.conn-off-note` and Connect (`goConnect`) filled by `renderConnUI`; Realistic photos shows `#photos-conn` only
- `addGate()` hides "Several items" while not connected and shows `#add-group-off`
- `applyRole()` (account.js) sets `body.owner`/`tester`/`nocon`: Advanced folds are owner-only; Connection's address and code hidden from testers
- `renderConnUI`/`connPaste`/`connSummary` draw Settings → Connection; `advToggle`/`advClose` the Advanced folds
- an ended invitation (`inviteOver()`: `invite_ended` holds the stored code) is not connected: `isConnected()` is false, so Add, Several items, item pictures and the server-only sections act as not connected; Connection's summary is "Invitation ended" and its box says so above the paste field; the Backup summary and line say nothing backs up (settings.js `backupSummary`, sync.js `showSyncStatus`); it said "Connected", "✓ … backed up" and "Backed up today" (2026-11-15)
- not connected, Connection's "Advanced · For the person who runs the server" fold is hidden until "I run the server: connect with its address and code" (`#conn-own`, `connOwn` → `body.srv-open`; styles.css `body.nocon:not(.srv-open) #adv-connection`): a new tester was invited to type into "Code"; tests click `#conn-own button` before the fold
- `plainErr(message)` turns server set-up errors (`TECH_RE`) into plain words unless the owner is connected; used by `tagFailText`, Several items, the picture batch and a tester's `setSyncStatus` (a raw OPENAI_API_KEY message once reached a new user)
- dialogs call `layerOpen`/`layerClose` (Settings, picker, What to buy); `layerInert` keeps the page behind inert and returns focus; `layerUp` traps Tab and handles Escape
- `LAYER_Q` is an array in z-index order (a question, the close-up chooser, `#kitsheet` (tour and import), the reading sheet, the color sheet, `#nps-sheet`, the picker, What to buy) and `layerUp` reads it in that order, not the page's (the no-photo sheet sits in main, before every other sheet; CR-9-02, 2026-11-17)
- with the no-photo sheet on top, `layerInert` leaves `main` reachable and calls `npsInert` (the rest of Add stays inert); its Escape is the shared listener's (`npsCancel`, not while it asks): the sheet's own listener never heard Escape once the focus fell to `<body>`
- the tour and the import sheet are in the Tab trap (Tab reached `<body>`); `kitImportOpen` focuses `#kitsheet` (`tabIndex=-1`, `outline:none`) (CR-9-03)
- `layerOpen` also keeps the opener's id (`layerFromId`): when the opener was drawn again while the dialog was up (What to buy's `analyzeWardrobe` redraws Insights' `#shop-btn`), `layerClose` focuses the new one instead of dropping the focus on `<body>` (CR-9-04)
- `tests/a11y.test.js` covers dialogs, waiting redraws, storage failures, labels, contrast, motion, tap targets
- a question takes a history entry (`backBorrow('ask')`, back.js) and the phone's Back answers it no (`askBack`), leaving the page under it open (fix round 2, 2026-11-15: Back closed Settings under "Roll back to …?" and left the question over Today)
- a real tap outside a question within `ASK_SETTLE` (450 ms) of its opening doesn't cancel it: it is the second tap of a double tap on the button that asked (`isTrusted`, so a test's `.click()` still cancels at once)
- `announce(text)` (in app.js, not ui.js: Today is drawn by the start-up chain, which can run before ui.js has loaded) writes `#sr-live` (emptied first, then written 60 ms later, so the same words are said again); `keepFocus(root, draw)` runs a redraw and, when the focused control inside `root` was replaced, focuses the one with the same id, or the same `onclick`/`onchange`/`oninput`/`data-k`/`data-c`/`data-s` (fix round 3, W2-19-01: focus fell to `<body>` after Next day, the pill, a pick, an Add card's Type); used by `renderToday`, `renderWardrobe`, `renderItem`'s body, `renderAdd`'s queue and sheet, `setupDraw`
- `keepFocus` never focuses a control whose focus opens the phone's own picker after a touch (CR-1-01, the owner's bug, 2026-11-16: on Add without a photo and in the item sheet every dropdown opened its picker again right after a choice, since iOS opens a `<select>`, or a date field's wheel, when it is focused): `focusOpens` (a select, or an input written as type date/time/month/week/datetime-local/color/file: the attribute, since a browser without date fields reports 'text'), `focusSafe` gives the section's head button (`.ckh`, which reads the new value) or else the field's `<label>` (tabindex -1); after a key press (`focusVia` 'key', from capture listeners `focusWatch` puts on the document the first time `keepFocus` runs) the new control itself keeps the focus, as before; `tests/pickerfocus.test.js` (Chromium and WebKit, touch: a tap then the choice in the real dropdown or date field, and by keyboard: Type, Material and Arrives on the no-photo card and in the item sheet)
- `layerInert` also makes `#item-view` inert while a layer above it is up (the color sheet, the close-up chooser, the reading sheet, a question, Settings); `askConfirm`/`askAnswer` call it; the Tab trap covers `#col-sheet` too (its Escape stays colpick.js's) (W2-19-03)
- a toast's words and button are taken out once it has faded (toast.js `toastOff`, 250 ms after `.on` goes), and it stays up while the keyboard is on its button (W2-19-06: an invisible Undo still worked 10 s later)

### `safety.js`

- data safety (loaded after app.js, before tagging.js and sync.js); `tests/safety.test.js`, `tests/safetysync.test.js`
- `storeFailure(e)`: marked by `txs`/`atomic` (`e.dwStore`), a `QuotaExceededError`, or an IndexedDB error event; `saveFailed` toasts it plainly (`storeFailText`) at most once per 1.5 s
- the `unhandledrejection` listener shows uncaught storage failures only; Add's `save()` relies on it and the card stays
- `dbDown`/`dbErr` come from safety.js's own `db.then`, queued before sync.js's, so the listener stays quiet when storage is down
- `storageDown(e)` (start-up chain's catch, start.js): `#nostore` over the page with the reason and Try again, the rest inert
- `askPersist()`: `navigator.storage.persist()` once (`persist_asked`, `persist_ok`), after an Add card is stored or from `safetyStart()`
- a tester's phone reports its errors by itself (2026-11-16, the owner: a tester's very first item failed and nothing showed why): `errNote(e, ctx)` notes the page too (`errWhere`) and `errSoon` sends the unsent entries of `err_log` to `POST /errors` 5 s later and at start-up (`errSend`: testers only and connected, `ERR_SEND_N` 10 a report, one report a minute, `ERR_SEND_MS`), marking them sent (`s`, kept for feedback); handled failures are noted with a context: "Photo not tagged" (`tagFailed`, not when offline), "Photo card", "Close-up reading" / "Close-up answer not applied", "Pattern reading" / "Pattern answer not applied" / "Pattern answer not drawn", "Try again", "Confirm on Add"; the worker keeps them in `errors:log` (worker/people.js `handleErrors`: 30 days, 400 at most, 20 reports a day per tester `EXTRA_CAPS.errors`, 100 for all, each one KV write), `GET /errors` is the owner's, deleting a tester deletes theirs (`dropTesterExtras`); Settings → About's feedback line tells testers (never photos or wardrobe data); read them on the PC with `node tools/read-errors.js [--name <tester>]` (DW_WORKER, DW_TOKEN); `tests/errreport.test.js`
- nothing fails silently (2026-11-14, after a tester's buttons did nothing on an iPhone): an error nobody caught (`error`, `unhandledrejection` that is not a storage failure) within `ERR_TAP_MS` (3 s) of a tap or Enter toasts "Something went wrong: … Try again; if it keeps happening, tell us in Settings → About → Feedback." (`errTell`, at most once in 5 s); every one, storage failures too, is noted in localStorage `err_log` (`errNote`: time, message, first stack line, app version; the last `ERR_MAX` 20; `errNoise` leaves out AbortError, the ResizeObserver loop warning and "Script error."); Send feedback (about.js) adds the last week's (`errReport`, at most 8) under "— Problems noted on this phone —" (`fbWithErrs`, within 4,000 characters) and clears the log once sent; `tests/errnet.test.js`
- a full WebKit run (`npm run test:webkit` with `TEST_ALL=1`, 2026-11-14) has about 50 failures that are the harness, not the app: no service worker, mouse wheel or clipboard permission in Playwright's Windows WebKit, mocked cross-origin routes refused ("access control checks"), no OffscreenCanvas WebGL in its worker, frame-timing checks, `translate(x)` serialised without its 0px, fixed waits too short for its slower planner; each app flow they cover was checked by hand there and works (the sample wardrobe and its removal, formal days, Settings colours, past days)
- `safetyStart()` (after `appReady`): `pruneTagImgs()` removes from `dw-keep-tagimg` the copies (`tagimg/<id>`, `closeimg/<id>`) of items no longer present; start-up only (undo window), never with an empty wardrobe
- tagging failures: `tagFailText` gives the Add note (`aiError`) and the reason (`tagWhy`), a 429's `message` as is; `tagFailed(q)` sets `q.tagErr`, drawn by `tagFailNote` (`.tagfail`) with Try again (`retryTag`)
- the reason (`tagReason`) is the server's `message`, then `detail`, in plain words (`plainErr`), one full stop (`oneStop`), its "Try again later, or add the item by hand." left out; a bare code ("mock", "busy") becomes "The photo couldn't be read just now"; a network failure sets `q.tagNet` (read again once online, add.js)
- `guardUnsaved()` (once, `guardOn`): `beforeunload` asks while `queue` holds an unsaved card
- `adoptBackupSettings`: on a device that never finished a sync (no `last_sync`), the backup's settings win key by key over newer local ones; device-only keys (`setupDone`) stay
- why: first-run setup stamps settings before connecting, and they replaced the backup's holidays, days off, colours and preferences

### `sync.js`

- sync-status.js and sync-photos.js are ES modules since 2026-11-23 (strict; `<script type="module" src="…">` at their old places, so sync-status.js still runs right before sync.js and sync-photos.js right after): their other names (`safariWord`, `safariTab`, `SAFARI_KEEP`, `nextUtcDay`, `endedTold`, the texts; `PH_OLD`, `PH_RE`, `PH_BATCH`, `isPhOld`, `phMemo`, `phHash`) are their own; sync-status.js bridges `setSyncStatus`, `showSyncStatus`, `ENDED`, `inviteEnded`, `endedAnswer`, `syncNetText`, `syncWaiting`, `pauseTillTomorrow`, `syncPaused`, `backupFullMsg`, `backedUpWords`; sync-photos.js bridges `phIn`, `phOut`, `phBatch` (sync.js) and `PH_MIN`, `isPh`, `phStrings`, `phMap` (tests); proved at the conversion (`C:\code\c2-mod3-lab\syncproof.js`, both engines, the real worker.js in Node): changes made offline, across a reload and while backups are paused are kept and merged, the same as main, request for request
- the backup's state is sync.js's since the restructure S3b (2026-11-26, moved from sync-status.js, the text unchanged but `setSyncStatus`'s last call): `setSyncStatus` (stores `sync_status`, then `emit('backup-changed')`), `syncNetText`, `syncWaiting`, `pauseTillTomorrow`, `syncPaused`, `backupFullMsg`, `backedUpWords`, `safariTab`, `tabBrowser`; sync-status.js keeps the Backup line (`showSyncStatus`, `safariWord`) and its tag comes right after sync.js's
#### Its files (Round B, 2026-11-17: split by job, no behavior change)
- five classic scripts in index.html's order and in sw.js `ASSETS`: `sync-status.js` (right before sync.js: `setSyncStatus`, `showSyncStatus`, `safariWord`/`safariTab`/`SAFARI_KEEP`, the ended-invitation texts and `inviteEnded`/`endedAnswer`/`endedTold`, `syncNetText`/`syncWaiting`, `nextUtcDay`/`pauseTillTomorrow`/`syncPaused`, `backupFullMsg`, `backedUpWords`), `sync.js` (the merge write, the first pull, `syncNow`/`syncOnce`/`syncLite`, the clock, the wipe question, the timers), `sync-photos.js` (`PH_MIN`/`PH_OLD`/`PH_RE`, `phHash`, `phIn`, `phOut`, `phBatch`), `snapshots.js` (the daily backups list, `rollBack`, `rollBackState`, `snapPreSetup`), `backupfile.js` (`downloadBackup`, `restoreBackup`, `restoreGone`)
- sync.js keeps its old place in the load order, so start-up finds `afterPull`/`pulledYet` exactly as before; sync-status.js before it, since `syncOnce` sets the status first thing; the other three hold only functions called during a sync or by a tap
- snapshots.js and backupfile.js are ES modules since 2026-11-23 (the ES-module move, step 4), in the same places: `snapPreSetup`, `rollSay`, `restoreGone` and their handlers are their own; `loadSnapshots`, `rollBack`, `rollBackState`, `snapLabel`, `downloadBackup` and `restoreBackup` are bridged onto `globalThis` (see index.html's section)
- `applyMerged(local, incoming)` merges (`mergeState`) and writes; callers no longer pass a pre-merged copy
- what of the sample wardrobe goes up is kit.js `kitShareFilter(st, remote, join)` (CR-5-10), with `join` = sync.js `syncJoinAt()`; sync.js reads no kit.js rule
- not done in Round B or C: the old-server path (CR-5-11, the owner's choice; it needs the server's logs)

#### Sync paths
- backup/sync through the worker's `/sync` (KV); backup file download/restore
- a change syncs without the whole backup (`syncLite`): `GET /sync?lite=1&since=<sync_mark's version>` brings only records changed since, photos as `dwph:<hash>`, every tombstone and `idx` (the backup's stamp of every record)
- with nothing changed here (`sync_mark`: backup version + wardrobe fingerprint) it adds `&v=<rev>:<saved>`, so an older worker answers `same` or the whole backup and the old way runs
- photos: taken from the device's own copy by hash (`phIn`) or downloaded (`GET /sync?ph=…`, `PH_BATCH` 40); `POST /sync {baseRev, baseSaved, day, reason?, delta, photos}` sends only records newer than `idx` and photos the backup lacks (`phOut`)
- a photo is any `data:` string of `PH_MIN` (16384)+ characters anywhere in a record, hashed as the first 20 hex of its SHA-256 (`phHash`, the same in worker/backup.js); a shorter one (the swatch) stays inline in the record (2026-11-15, the owner's option A; it was 256)
- strings from `PH_OLD` (256, the rule before) may still be references in records an older app or worker saved (a PNG swatch): `phIn` matches them to this device's copy and takes them when downloaded (`isPhOld`); `tests/backup-storage.test.js`
- measured on the owner's 16.7 MB backup: a day marked worn went from 16.7 MB each way to 15 KB down and 0.3 KB up
- missing something `idx` says the backup holds → asks for everything (`all`), so a lost wardrobe comes back; 409 → asks again; 400 `missing` → sends those photos; 422 `legacy` (over 20 MB, stored by an older app) → the old way
- the full PUT body is `{baseRev, day, reason?, data}` with `data` last (the worker reads a large backup's leading fields without parsing it)
- `window.SYNC_OLD` before load syncs the old way (`SYNC_LITE` false)
- `syncOnce` calls `adoptBackupSettings` (safety.js) before merging
- `tests/backup-storage.test.js`: two devices and an older app, every kind of change under 100 KB each way, against the old worker (tests/fixtures/old-worker/worker-ef18d06.js)
- photos go up in batches (2026-11-16, the audit's W2-18-01: a restored file on a new account sent one 61 MB POST at every close, refused 413 over `BACKUP_MAX`, storing nothing): `phBatch` puts items into one POST until the next item's new photos would pass `SYNC_SEND_MAX` (16 MiB; one item always goes); days, settings and deletions go in the first batch; after a batch the next round asks `since` the version it made (`again.batch`, up to `SYNC_BATCH_MAX` 40 rounds a sync, not counted as retries); the status reads "Backing up: N items with photos still to send…" (`showSyncStatus`, Backup summary "Backing up photos"); works with the worker as it was (plain partial deltas)
- a 413 halves the budget (`syncSendMax`) and tries again; one item still too large, or a full PUT answered 413, pauses uploads until the next UTC day with `TOO_BIG_SYNC`, so nothing is resent at every close; a roll-back's first batch keeps "Before the last roll-back" and ends `sync_restore` (`restoreDone`), so later batches don't replace that copy
- `window.SYNC_SEND_TEST` before load sets a smaller budget for a test; `tests/backup-storage.test.js`
- a storage failure on this phone during a sync (full, closed) is said in the app's words in a toast (`syncStoreTell`, every time: through `saveFailed`'s once-in-1.5 s it was skipped on joining, a start-up write's toast having just been replaced by "Connected as …") and the status ("Backup failed: your phone’s storage is full…"), not the browser's (W1-11-05); app.js `atomic` aborts with the error a `put` throws at once, not WebKit's "IDBTransaction will abort due to uncaught exception"
- the daily backups list names each copy by the day it is for, with its item count (`snapLabel`: "Mon, Nov 16, start of the day · 5 items", "… · empty", "Start of November 2026 · 2 items"; W2-14-03, W1-6-07: two "Sun, Nov 15, 7:30 AM" entries, one full, one empty); the count is the worker's `n` (copies made before have none); an empty copy's roll-back question says it empties the wardrobe
- a daily copy is kept only before a day's first real change: a save the server takes nothing from (since #485, future-dated deletion marks are dropped, so the audit's W2-13-03 attack writes nothing) keeps no copy, and the list can be empty after it (checked 2026-11-16 with the lab's clock moved forward: copies for each changed day, a phone date a day ahead of UTC included, listed with their counts; `tests/backup-storage.test.js`)
- `backedUpWords()`: what Connection (ui.js) and the setup's Connection step say about backups; while `sync_pause` it says backups are paused until the reset and the newest changes are only on this phone (W1-10-04)
- the Backup summary says "Paused until <time>" when the next 00:00 UTC is still today here, "Paused until tomorrow" otherwise (settings.js `pausedUntil`, W1-10-03)
- in a Safari tab on an iPhone or iPad (`safariTab`: kit.js `homeIOS`, not `homeStandalone`) a backup pause adds that Download backup keeps a copy meanwhile (`SAFARI_KEEP`, W1-11-06), and the unconnected status adds why the file matters (`safariWord`)
- `downloadBackup` keeps the time in `localStorage.backup_file_at` (kit.js `safariRemind`)

#### First pull and locks
- a connected device saves nothing at start-up until a sync has merged the backup (`afterPull`, `pulledYet`, `pullDone`, `whenPulled`): Today's history and locks, arrivals, `texSwatches`, `recolourSpecSetts`, `upgradeCloseups`, `upgradePhotos`, `smallPhotos`; not connected, an ended invitation or no backup (501): at once; offline: they wait
- why: a phone two days behind locked the week and re-saved 82 items before syncing; their newer stamps replaced another device's pick and tags and brought back deleted items
- a start-up fix-up saves an item only while it is still the copy in `items` (`stillCur`, app.js), and redraws through `fixupRedraw` (never with Settings open, since `renderWardrobe` closes it)
- the week's locks are marked as the app's own (`lockOnly`, `base`; only `saveDay(rec, quiet, true)` keeps the mark): in a merge (`dayWins` in core.js `mergeState`, worker/backup.js `mergeLite`) a newer automatic copy loses to an older one changed after its base
- such a kept day is sent up by the small sync (`keptD`) and kept by the worker, so devices settle; `tests/staledevice.test.js` replays it
- `tests/staledevice.test.js` runs its devices on a Sunday 10:00 one to two weeks back (Date shifted by an init script, the worker's `x-now` by the same amount, so no clock gap is learned): the lock runs only through Friday, so on a Thursday or Friday there was no coming workday to lock and its wait timed out (deploy blocked, Friday 2026-11-20); it reads the day's plan from the day's own month (`planFor` is the month on screen, which failed when the coming day was the 1st)

#### Timing, limits, errors
- a tester's phone backs up on going to the background or closing, or 15 min after the first waiting change (`TESTER_SYNC_MS`, `syncDue`); the owner's 2.5 s after a change
- over a daily backup limit nothing uploads until the next UTC day (`sync_pause`, `sync_pause_msg`), with the server's sentence; connecting again clears it
- the worker's daily KV writes used up (Cloudflare free plan: 1,000 a day, reset 00:00 UTC; the PC's picture uploads once used them up): 429 `{error:'kv-limit'}` (`kvFull`, worker.js), the same pause, and "Today's backups are used up on the server…" (`backupFullMsg`); `tests/server-limits.test.js`
- an invitation switched off (403 `invite off`): said once (`inviteEnded`, `localStorage.invite_ended`) and syncing stops
- nothing more is asked with an ended code: photos.js `workerBase()` is null while `inviteOver()` (the photo list, item and outfit pictures, the masters, holiday lists), app.js `ask()` sends no photo, tagging.js `claudePattern` and add.js `addMulti`/`fetchPhoto` don't call; a photo answered 403 'invite off' marks the invitation ended (`endedAnswer` → `inviteEnded`); the first open after an end still sends what runs before the sync learns of it (re-audit 2026-11-15: 12–45 refused requests per open)
- the one-time message of an ended invitation (`endedTold`): a replaced link says the new one opens the same wardrobe (`ENDED_NEW`), a deleted account says the backup was deleted (`ENDED_GONE`, W1-6-03), else `ENDED`; status lines keep `ENDED`
- `endedAnswer(status, j)`: 403 'invite off', or 403 'forbidden token' on a tester's phone (the account was deleted on another phone or by the owner), is an ended invitation for the sync, the daily backups list, Feedback and `loadAccount`; it said "Backups aren’t available on this server yet" and kept trying (2026-11-15)
- sync errors show the server's `message` before its `error`

#### The phone's clock (fix round 3, 2026-11-16, the audit's W1-9-01..08, W1-5-07, W2-13-03)
- every answer of the worker carries its time (`x-now`, exposed by CORS); each sync's GET and save learns the gap (`clockLearn`: the server's time less the request's middle; under `CLOCK_MIN` 3 s it is 0) into `localStorage.clock_skew` and core.js `clockSkew`
- `nowMs()` (core.js) is the phone's time corrected by that gap; every stamp uses it: items' `updated`/`added` (app.js `put`/`del`, add.js, kit.js, shop.js), days (history.js `saveDay`, the week's locks and `lockAt`), settings (`stampSettings`), holidays' `fetched`, the roll-back, the tombstone cutoff
- a new stamp is always above the one it replaces (`nextStamp(prev)`): an item's edit, its deletion mark (`del` returns it, the Wardrobe's Undo goes one above), a day, a setting's key; so an edit or a deletion made on a copy wins over that copy whatever the clocks say, also before any server has answered
- a gap of an hour or more is said once (`clockTell`, `clock_told`): "Your phone’s clock is 1 year ahead. Your changes are dated by the right time anyway. To fix the clock, turn on Set Automatically…"
- once a sync has learned the gap, stamps on the phone over `CLOCK_AHEAD` (10 min) past the corrected time are put back to now (`clockFix`, before the merge; the sync then asks again): items, days (`updated`, `base`, `lockAt`), tombstones, settings' `ks`/`updated`; a holiday list `fetched` ahead is fetched again (0)
- dates by the corrected time: past days are recorded only before `trueToday()` (history.js `recordDays`), no day is recorded for today and no week locked while the phone's date is ahead of it (`lockUpcoming`), the holiday window (`fetchHolidays`), a list fetched ahead is due (`refreshHolidays`), the backup pause (`nextUtcDay`, `syncPaused`: a pause over 26 h away is let go), the daily copy's `day`
- a roll-back stamps each restored item above this phone's deletion mark and copy of it, each day above its copy, and the settings above every key (`rollBackState`)
- measured KV writes (lab, a tester phone; before → after): setup day 4 → 4, a light day 2 → 2, an Add day (3 pieces) 2 → 2; `tests/clock.test.js`

#### Daily backups
- before the first PUT of each day the worker keeps the backup as `snap:<YYYY-MM-DD>` (30 days) and `snap:<YYYY-MM>` (400 days); `GET /sync?snaps=1` lists, `?snap=<id>` returns one
- "Roll back" (`loadSnapshots`/`rollBack`/`rollBackState`) restamps the snapshot's records and tombstones the rest, so it wins on every device; the PUT carries `reason:'restore'` and the worker keeps `snap:before-restore`
- a roll-back's upload is marked in localStorage before the phone's copy changes (`sync_restore` = the code; `restoreWanted`/`restoreDone`): every upload until one goes through carries `reason:'restore'`, so a kill or reload mid-roll-back still gets "Before the last roll-back" kept at the next start (2026-11-15, the new-user audit: it was memory only and 24 pieces were lost for good); `tests/rollback.test.js`
- what a roll-back says (`rollSay`) goes to Settings' line and, when Settings isn't open (closed under its question by Back, or the toast's Undo), to a toast too: a success has Undo, which asks and rolls back to "Before the last roll-back" (`rollBack({id, label})`); written only to the closed Settings page the wardrobe vanished from Today with no word (re-audit 2026-11-15); `tests/rollback.test.js`
- a copy from before the setup (`snapPreSetup`: no `setupDone`, no items, and this phone's setup done) brings back no settings: the setup started again at Welcome with the workdays and location gone; the message says the settings were kept
- the daily backups list is read when Settings' Backup opens (`setCatOpen` in settings.js → `loadSnapshots`), not only on the dropdown's focus (an iPhone opens the picker at the tap, with only "Choose a day…"); once per page life (each list is a counted server call), one request at a time (`snapLoading`), the chosen day kept
- the list cap (429 `what:'list'`) is said as `SNAP_CAP` ("The daily backups can’t be listed again today…"), not the server's "photo list checks"
- settings a synced copy marks removed (`kh` '-': a roll-back, a removal elsewhere) leave memory too (`takeSettings` in core.js, used wherever stored or downloaded settings are taken: start.js, safety.js, daysoff.js): a roll-back's removed day off and holidays stayed in memory and the next change stamped them back on every phone

#### Messages
- a sync that brings changes while Settings is open redraws its controls (`syncSettingsRedraw`: `renderSettingsExtras`; while a text field in it is focused, once it is left)
- a backup that never reached the server says so in words (`syncNetText`: "Not backed up: you’re offline…" / "…the server couldn’t be reached…", `syncWaiting`), with the last backup's time; the `online` event backs up again; `netErr` (ui.js) turns "Load failed" / "Failed to fetch" into plain words inside `plainErr` for everyone (picture batch, Several items, roll-back and backup lists)
- the Backup summary (`backupSummary`, kept current by `showSyncStatus`): "Paused until tomorrow" while `sync_pause`, "Waiting for a connection", "Not backed up: ask for a new invitation link", and "Backed up today · newer changes waiting" while `sync_wait` (set by `scheduleSync`, cleared by `synced`)
- a tester's code the server no longer knows (403 `forbidden token`: storage lost, account deleted) ends the invitation (`endedAnswer`, `inviteEnded(token, answer)`) and is also marked `localStorage.invite_lost`: a new invitation link then asks "Back up this phone’s wardrobe…" and keeps the wardrobe instead of "Clear and switch" (account.js `connectFromLink`; 2026-11-15)
- the write ceiling is worded by the app (`backupFullMsg`: `pr.write`, `pr.all` → "everyone’s backups"), without the server's photo count
- feedback refused shows the server's `message` (or an `error` that is a sentence), never a code like `kv-limit` (about.js `sendFeedback`)

#### Redraws
- a sync's redraw waits while the item sheet or piece picker is open (`syncRedraw` → `syncLater`; `syncLaterRun` on `itemShown(false)` or `closePicker`)
- …and while a finger is on Today (fix round A, CR-4-11): `todayBusy()` (today.js: a swipe under way, a month cell held or an outfit being moved, a swipe's fly-out not drawn yet); `todayFree()` runs the waiting redraw when the gesture ends; drawing Today removed the element the touch began on and the gesture stopped; `tests/todayedges.test.js`
- a sync's redraw of Insights leaves What to buy open over it (fix round A, CR-4-04): `show()` closes the sheet only when the tab really changes (`from!==t`); `tests/shopstay.test.js`
- the monthly holiday refetch (`refreshHolidays`, daysoff.js) syncs first and refetches only if still needed, never offline: its newest stamp undid a newer change from elsewhere
- the Backup line (`backupSummary`, settings.js; `showSyncStatus`) shows the last backup, a failure, or "Not backed up yet", and keeps the last backup while the next runs ("… · syncing…")

#### Field-by-field merge and deletions elsewhere (fix round 3, 2026-11-16)
- `applyMerged` checks deletions with core.js `tombWins` and tells the phone when an edit it made after a piece was deleted elsewhere wasn't kept ("“X” was deleted on another phone, so your change to it wasn’t kept."); with the item sheet open it calls item-view.js `itemSynced` with the copy shown before
- `syncLite`'s "lacks" check doesn't count an item the backup sent that a deletion here outweighs (`dead`): an older worker keeps it by its stamp alone, and the app would ask for everything at every sync
- the owner's "Delete everything" on another phone (W1-6-01): the worker keeps `wiped` {at} (people.js `handleAccount`, `WIPED_KEY`) and `GET /sync?lite=1` answers `wiped` for the owner; `syncWiped`: a phone whose `last_sync` is before it merges and sends nothing, says `WIPED_SYNC` ("Backups held: …"; the Backup summary "Held: deleted from the server on another device") and asks once "Back it up again" (localStorage `wipe_seen` = that time, then it syncs: everything goes up) / "Not now" (nothing sent, asked again only from Sync now, `wipeLater`); a phone that never synced, or synced after it, goes on as usual; a tester's other phones stop as before (the invitation is gone)
- Restore file: the file's settings are spread over this phone's and all stamped now (`stampAllSettings`), so they win (W1-11-03: the new setup's vest choice and holiday country won the per-setting merge after a wipe); items and days merge as usual
- Restore file names the file's pieces that stayed deleted, up to three ("1 piece in the file stayed deleted, since it was deleted after the file was saved: “Burgundy tie”…", `restoreGone`; CR-5-03: it said only "Restored"); whether Restore should become a roll-back is the owner's call
- `applyMerged` writes each item and day as the merge of what storage holds now with the incoming copy (`mergeItem(cur,it)`, `mergeDay(cur,d)` inside its `atomic`), and only when the result differs from `cur`; it decided by stamps alone, so a day whose winner was the older copy (another phone's formal day beating this phone's own week lock, `dayWins`) was never written and the phones disagreed for good (CR-5-01); an edit made during a sync is now merged field by field instead of skipped; `tests/syncwrite.test.js`
- one sync at a time (CR-5-04): `syncNow` while a run is going returns one run queued after it (`syncAgain`), shared by every call that came meanwhile; five calls at once started three `syncOnce` before
- `last_sync` is stamped with `nowMs()`, and the backup pause is compared with `nowMs()` (`backedUpWords`, `backupSummary`, the foreground sync's 60 s): a phone whose clock was behind had its backups held by `syncWiped` (CR-5-06, CR-5-15)
- measured KV writes for these sync changes (lab, a tester phone; main → this change): setup day 1 → 1, a light day 3 → 3, an Add day (3 pieces) 2 → 2
- a save's refusals are handled once for the POST and the PUT (`syncSaved`; `pauseTillTomorrow(msg)` for every pause)
- `applyMerged` writes the settings as `mergeSettings(cur, merged)` with the stored copy inside one `atomic('meta','settings',…)`, only when the result is not `cur` (Round C, the rest of CR-5-09, the owner's approval): by stamps alone a setting changed here during a sync kept the stored copy whole, and the other phone's setting was missing on this phone until a later sync; `tests/syncwrite.test.js`
- with nothing changed during the sync the write is what it was (checked on 20,000 random setting pairs, split settings included: the same values and stamps, or the stored copy kept when it already equals them)
- `syncJoinMark`'s `sync_join.at`, `scheduleSync`'s `sync_wait` and a tester's `syncDue` (15 minutes) are by `nowMs()` (Round C, CR-5-15): kit.js `kitShareFilter` compares the join time with days' `nowMs()` stamps; `tests/syncwrite.test.js` checks them on a phone an hour behind
- measured KV writes for these (lab, a tester's phones; main → this change, the same in two runs each): setup day 7 → 7, a light day with a setting changed 3 → 3, an Add day (3 pieces) 8 → 8, a day two phones each changed a different setting 3 → 3
#### Records from outside (fix round 3)
- `restoreBackup`, `syncLite` (after `phIn`), the whole sync and `rollBack` run core.js `cleanState` on what came in; `syncLite` and the whole sync run it on what they send (the delta only, in the small sync)
- Restore file says how many items it left out ("Restored. Your wardrobe now has 6 items. 2 items in the file weren’t a Daily Wardrobe item and were left out.")
- a record the check left out of a sync is remembered per server address (localStorage `sync_off` {url, i, d}, 500 each) so `syncLite`'s "lacks" test doesn't ask for the whole backup at every sync because the server still holds it
- app.js `cleanStored` (start-up, once per phone: `rec_clean` '1', set once every write went through): a stored record the check changes is written back with its stamp unchanged (no upload; other phones clean their own), one with no usable id or type is taken out of storage
- nothing is dropped silently: before any stored record is changed or taken out, every original is added to meta `rec_kept` ([{kind, dropped, rec, at}], appended, never replaced), and a one-time toast says so (`recKeptTell`: "N items in this phone’s storage couldn’t be shown safely and were set aside. … Nothing was deleted: the originals are kept on this phone.")
- per-field stamps (`item.fs`, `day.fs`: field or slot → stamp) pass the check unchanged (plain words as keys, finite numbers), and settings (`ks`/`kh`, `kse`/`khe`) are never touched by it; the server's `recCheck` takes them too; `tests/records.test.js`
- weave, patternSpec, tagAI, meas and away keep their prose as it is, quotes included (core.js `recProse`), only `< > \`` taken out, and a value under a `hex` key must be a hex color (patternSpec.across bands reach a fill); worker/records.js `proseOk` is the same rule
- checked 2026-11-16 over 6,007 stored-shape items and 51,012 days in the audits' data (lab KV stores with photos as references, the 12 Nov build's copies, the 14 Nov new-user backup with own colors, backups with photos inline, 590 sample pieces) and in a browser start-up pass over 102 items (sample saved the app's way with sampleMine, own colors and patterns with digits, close-ups, band/motif/texture readings, patternSpec and weave prose with quotes, a picture record): the check changed nothing but the crafted records (`C:\code\w3-fix1-lab\realdata.js`, `startpass.js`)
- the second lock, at the markup: ids inside inline handlers go through `jsq` (photos.js: JS-escaped, then `esc`), ids in attributes through `esc`, item and picture sources through `srcOk` (a data:image base64 picture, a blob: or http(s) address or a file path with nothing that ends the attribute, else empty), in wardrobe.js, itemsheet.js, today.js, photos.js, add.js, history.js, care.js, colpick.js, stats.js and kit.js
- photos.js `keepRender` keeps a picture record only when its `img` is a whole `REC_PHOTO` string (W2-13-06); assemble.js `asmPre` takes a server picture only of that shape
- tagging.js `confTxt` shows only the words medium and low (W2-13-05); item names typed or imported lose bidi override and isolate characters on save (wardrobe.js `setItemField`, `wardSaveFields`), and the owner's Feedback received shows feedback without them (about.js) (W2-12-06)
- checked for backup data reaching markup (2026-11-16): Today (pieces, tiles, swatches, the picture), the Wardrobe grid and item sheet, the piece picker, Insights, What to buy, the month, packing, Laundry, Settings' lists and the pictures; names and other free text were already escaped everywhere, the planner's words (`desc`, `nm`) are built from keys only
- settings at the markup (fix round A, 2026-11-17): Settings' own colors and patterns (tagging.js `renderCustomUI`: Remove's `data-rm-kind`/`data-rm-key`, only entries of the own-key rule listed, the hex escaped), Days off (daysoff.js `daysOffListen`: a holiday's checkbox `data-d`, a day off's × `data-ds`) and What to buy's "Show again" (shop.js `data-unhide`) carry their values in data-* attributes read by one listener each, not in inline handlers (`esc` inside one is decoded back before it runs)
- `tests/inlinehandlers.test.js` (a lint) fails on a quoted `${…}` inside an inline handler that isn't `jsq(…)` and isn't on its list of values checked to be app-made (ids, slot names, the app's own keys); a new one goes into data-* or `jsq`, or is checked and listed
- checked for settings reaching markup (fix round A): the location name (escaped or `textContent`), holiday and day-off names (`esc`, `nameHtml`), Today's and the month's day-off line (`esc`), the packing list (`nameHtml`), Care's limits and laundry days (numbers), the dress code and picture switches (fixed values), own materials (`ownMats`, `jsq`), the tagging card (counts now numbers, corrections escaped), workdays (looked up in `DAY_NAMES`), the color and pattern pickers (`CUSTOM_COL`/`CUSTOM_PAT` keys of the own-key rule)
- daysoff.js `fetchHolidays`' `tidy` keeps only a holiday with a `YYYY-MM-DD` date and a name, the name cleaned like `cleanSettings`' (`setStr`, 120 letters): its string compare let `2026-12-01" onclick="…` through from Nager.Date, the server or the built-in lists

### `account.js`

- who the app is connected as (after sync.js); `acct` holds `GET /me`
- since the restructure S3b (2026-11-26) `acct`, the connection (`acctBase`, `acctCall`, `acctEsc`), the role (`rememberRole`, `acctRole`, `isTester`, `applyRole`), `hasInviteHash` and an ended invitation (`ENDED`, `endedAnswer`, `inviteEnded`) are who.js's (loaded right after srgb.js); account.js keeps the invitation link (`connectFromLink`, `acctWhy`, `inviteChecking`, `acctFailText`) and the account box, assigns `acct` with `setAcct`, and redraws on `invite-ended` (bus.js)
- invite link `<app url>#invite=<code>&w=<server>`; `connectFromLink` stores `worker_url`/`app_token` only when `/me` accepts; `acctStart()` runs it for `#invite=` at start-up
- a refused link changes nothing; `#invite` is dropped from the address unless the server was unreachable
- a link on a phone connected with another code: with a local wardrobe (`acctLocal`, read from the database) it asks, wipes (`wipeDevice`) and reloads; else it switches and drops `sync_mark`, `last_sync`, `sync_status` (a sync once merged the owner's wardrobe into a tester's)
- Connection's `#acct-box`: `renderAccountUI`, `loadAccount` (at most once in 3 s); testers see use against caps (`ACCT_CAPS`: item pictures, photo readings, pattern readings; `acctUse`), the owner invites (`createInvite`, `copyInvite`, `revokeInvite`, `purgeInvite`)
- `acctStart()` registers `acctSettingsPart` (the account box, `loadAccount`) with settings.js `settingsPart`, so every Settings redraw draws it (Round B, CR-7-11: it wrapped `renderSettingsExtras`); an old server (404/405 on `/me`) leaves the box empty
- the role is remembered per code (`rememberRole`, `acct_role`, `acctRole()`, `isTester()`)
- iPhone: a Home Screen app has its own storage; a tester connecting in Safari (`iosInSafari`) gets `homeScreenGuide()` and "Copy my invitation link" (`copyInviteLink`, `myInviteLink`; testers only)
- a daily limit shows the server's `message`: through `detail` on Add, thrown by `renderItemPic`, toasted for pattern reading via `limitNote`
- testers see no costs (`showCost()` in settings.js)
- `ACCT_CAPS` includes "Colour & pattern lookups" (`describe`, 2026-11-15)
- About's Feedback line (`#fb-help`) says where feedback goes to testers only
- tests: `tests/accounts.test.js`, `tests/server-limits.test.js`, `tests/invite.test.js`
- `connectFromLink` decides only after storage is read (2026-11-15, the new-user audit's Critical): it awaits `connRestored` and counts the wardrobe in the database (`acctLocal`: items, day records, sample pieces); at start-up the link was checked before `items` was loaded, the phone looked empty, it switched without asking and the next sync copied its wardrobe into the other account; `acctHasLocal` is gone; `tests/invite.test.js` holds the database's first reads back 1.5 s to reproduce it
- one link at a time (`acctLinkRun`); `opt.address` (the page's own address) says the outcome in a toast, the paste fields (Settings `connPaste`, setup `setupConnect`) say it in their own line from `acctWhy` ('bad', 'refused' with `acctWhyMsg`, 'offline', 'kept') via `acctFailText()`: offline is no longer "check that you copied all of it"
- a `#invite=` link that can't be read says "That invitation link didn’t work…" (`INVITE_BAD`); a full stop, comma or bracket after the link is ignored (`inviteParts`)
- a link opened or pasted in the tab already showing the app is read (`hashchange`); a link the server couldn't be reached for stays in the address and is tried again on `online` and on coming to the front (`acctRetryLink`); Settings' Connection and the setup's Connection step say so, with the link in the field
- while a link from the address is checked (`inviteChecking`) Connection, its summary and the setup's Connection and All set steps say "Checking your invitation…"; `acctLinkDrawn()` redraws them, Settings' summaries and Add when the check ends
- connecting (not switching) with sample pieces on the phone asks nothing and keeps them (re-audit 2026-11-15): the sample is never backed up (kit.js `kitShareFilter`), so it can't reach the person's wardrobe or his other phones; it used to ask "Remove the sample wardrobe first?"
- (fix round 2) the Home Screen sheet after a link (`homeScreenGuide`) shows once per phone (`homeTold`, `localStorage.home_told`) and never when the phone was already connected with that code: it covered the app every time a tester tapped his link; About's button still opens it
- a successful link clears `invite_ended`; `loadAccount` marks an ended invitation (`endedAnswer`, sync.js) instead of showing "The server didn’t accept this connection" to a tester; `acctEndedUI()` redraws what said Connected
- (re-audit 2026-11-15, N1) a new link on a phone whose code has ended, switched off (`invite_ended`) as well as lost (`invite_lost`), asks "Back up this phone’s wardrobe with …’s invitation?" ("Keep it and back it up") and keeps the wardrobe, changes since the end included, instead of "Clear and switch": the old backup can't be reached, and after a switch-off the app stops syncing so it never hears of a later deletion; `tests/invite.test.js`
- "Clear and switch" first backs up what no backup took (`acctPending`: `syncMark().sig` against the wardrobe's `stateSig`, then `syncNow()`); still pending (backups paused, server refusing), the question says the changes would be lost and its button reads "Clear, losing those changes" (`acctSwitchText`); it also says the settings go and the setup starts again
- (fix round 3, W2-13-01/W2-13-04) a link is followed only to the app's own server (`acctOwnServer`): `APP_SERVERS` (the published worker's origin; someone hosting the app elsewhere adds theirs), the origin this phone is already connected to, and, only for a page served from this computer, `*.test` and loopback on the page's port + 1 (the tests and the audit's lab); any other `w=` is refused before anything is asked or sent ("…a server this app doesn’t use…", `INVITE_STRANGER`); a `w=` over `INVITE_W_MAX` (300) characters or with a name/password is unreadable (W2-13-11)
- (fix round 3, W1-7-01) before a switch the server is asked about the old code (`GET /me` with it): a 403 'invite off' (switched off, replaced) or 'forbidden token' (deleted, storage lost) is an ended invitation (the N1 "Keep it and back it up" question), as is what `invite_ended`/`invite_lost` remembered; unreachable, 404 or 405 fall back to those
- (CR-5-02, 2026-11-17) any other answer about the old code (a 5xx, 429, another 403) changes nothing and asks nothing ("Couldn’t check the invitation this phone uses now…", `ACCT_OLD_UNSURE`): a single 503 was read as ended, and "Keep it and back it up" copied a live account's whole wardrobe into the other one; `tests/invite.test.js`
- the same account under a new code (`acctIdFor`: `rememberRole` now keeps the account's `id` in `acct_role`, or the old code's `/me`) switches with no question and nothing cleared, sync marks kept, toast "…: the same wardrobe as before" (the owner's new link for a tester, decision 1)
- an empty phone connected with another code asks "Switch this phone to …’s invitation?" naming its connection (`acctSwitchEmptyText`, W2-13-04); a phone not connected with pieces of its own (sample not counted) asks "Add this phone’s N pieces to …’s wardrobe?" ("Add and connect"; W1-7-03, W1-11-01)
- the switch question for the owner's phone (old `/me` role owner) says how to come back (Settings → Connection → "I run the server", `acctComeBack`), and says the setup starts again only if the other account has no wardrobe (W1-7-04); after the switch `localStorage.acct_was_owner` keeps "I run the server" in Connection on that tester-connected phone (ui.js `renderConnUI`, styles.css `body.tester:not(.srv-open) #adv-connection`; W1-7-02)
- another address or code typed under Advanced while connected goes through the link's questions (`acctTyped` from settings.js `saveSettings`), so a typed code never merges this phone's wardrobe into another account silently
- a first connection typed under Advanced goes through `acctTyped` too (CR-5-07): the code is checked with `/me` before it is stored, "Add this phone’s N pieces…?" is asked, and the role is remembered
- the owner's list has "New link" on each active tester (`reissueInvite`, POST /invites {reissue: id}): a new link that opens the same wardrobe, shown once ("New link for …"); the old link stops working
- the tester's "Copy my invitation link" line says to keep the link somewhere safe: it brings the wardrobe back after the phone's data is cleared (W1-11-02)
- tests: `tests/invite.test.js`
- the backup before a switch (`acctBackUpFirst`) runs up to `ACCT_SYNC_TRIES` (3) times while the server takes it, since its first pull lets the start-up writers save the day's record, and gives up after `ACCT_SYNC_MS` (20 s), so a slow connection still gets the question

### `setup.js`

- the Connection step's words on the backup come through the bus.js socket `backed-up-words` (sync.js serves `backedUpWords`; the restructure S3f, 2026-11-26): without sync.js the step says "your wardrobe is backed up", as ui.js's Connection line does and as main showed before S3b moved `backedUpWords` into sync.js (S3b's main threw there and the step drew the Weather step again; `C:\code\s3f-lab\connfail.js`, both engines)
- first-run setup and empty states (loaded last): `#setup`, built by `setupEl()`, not in index.html
- `setupStart()` (start.js chain) opens it for an empty wardrobe with no recorded day and no `settings.setupDone`
- with items it never shows: `setupDone` is set in memory only and stored with the next settings change (storing it at once would force a full sync)
- steps `SETUP_STEPS` (welcome, week, wear, weather, connect, done) via `setupStep`, `setupGo`, `setupDraw`; Skip finishes at once
- steps store through the app's own functions (`toggleDay`, `setUnits`, `setVestMode`, `setLocation`, `useMyLocation`, `findCity`)
- `setupSearch` uses Settings' `#cfg-city` and `window._cities`; `setupLocate` polls `settings.location`
- leaving the Weather step with an unfinished city search warns once (`setupCityOpen`, `cityWarned`)
- What you wear: `settings.dress = {jacket, tie}` (`setupSetDress`, `setupDress`; missing = true), releasing the week's locks (`clearLocks`)
- a new person who doesn't choose gets `vestMode='never'` (`setupFinish`, self-opened setup only); the stored default stays `auto`
- Connection: `setupConnected`, else an invite link field (`connectFromLink`; the fallback `setupParseLink` is gone, CR-7-14)
- (fix round 3, W1-11-01) not connected, the Welcome (`#su-before`) and the Connection step say an invitation link opened again brings the backed-up wardrobe back
- `setupFinish` stores `setupDone` via `saveUserSettings`; the last step lists workdays Sunday first and asks for trousers only with jackets on and no suit
- picks the time zone country's holidays (`setupHolidays`, `tzCountry`, `TZ_CC`); without a list yet the country is kept with `fetched` 0 and `refreshHolidays` retries
- default workdays Sunday to Thursday (`settings.workdays` [0..4]): the owner's choice
- "Run setup again" (`#setup-again`) → `setupOpen(true)`, keeping the stored vest choice
- a self-opened setup steps aside when items arrive or another tab opens (`setupYield`, from `show()` and `setupToday`)
- while up the page is inert (`setupInert`); z-index 980, under Settings
- under `navigator.webdriver` it opens only with `window.SETUP_AUTO=true` (`setupAuto`), since every test starts as a new install
- Today's checklist (`setupToday`, rows from `setupNeeds`): suit or blazer, shirt, tie, trousers ("If no suit" with jackets on and no suit), shoes; goes in place of the picture, above the score card or under the note depending on how dressed the day is
- empty Wardrobe: `setupWardEmpty()` (`#wempty`)
- `tests/setup.test.js` covers the flow, both connection paths, stepping aside, both checklist kinds, and layout at 320/390/626px
- (audit 2026-11-15) the step on screen is kept in `localStorage.setup_at` (`setupKeep`/`setupResume`: `{k, again, before, vestSet}`), so a setup iOS cut off opens again on that step; removed by `setupClose`
- the new-person "No vest" applies only when the vest has no stamp (`setupVestChosen`: `setStampOf(settings,'vestMode')`), so a choice made before a kill, or the account's from another phone, stays
- the vest row shows only with jackets and ties (as Settings); the dress switches keep other `settings.dress` keys (jeans)
- "Run setup again" has Cancel instead of Skip (`setupCancel`): it puts back `SETUP_UNDO` (workdays, blazer days, dress, vest, units, location) as they were at open (`setup.before`, kept with the step)
- a tap at the spot of the tap that closed the setup within `SETUP_TAP_MS` (350) is dropped (`setupTapGuard`): a double tap on Skip opened Settings, one on "Add my first item" skipped the tour's first card; tests wait past it
- connected at the setup's own Connection step (`setup.joined`, or while `setup.busy`), items arriving don't close it: it goes on to All set with the account's answers (adoptBackupSettings) and says the wardrobe came across
- All set lists the blazer days and the vest; its "Try a sample wardrobe" (`setupSample`) finishes the setup first (holidays, vest)
- `setupOpen` closes a Home Screen kit sheet (one explanation)
- `tests/setup.test.js` covers these and the Settings fixes of the same audit
- (fix round 2) a cut-off "Run setup again" (`setup_at` with `again`) opens again at start-up whatever `setupNo` says (start-up's `show('today')` sets it whenever there are items, so it never came back for anyone with a wardrobe); one that can't be opened then (no `setupDone`, Settings open, a test without `SETUP_AUTO`) is dropped, so it never opens by itself later with a Cancel that would undo settings changed since
- (fix round 2) a double tap on Get started / Next / Back steps once (`setupGoDouble`: a real tap within `SETUP_TAP_MS` at the last step tap's spot is dropped; calls from code always step); tests wait 400 ms between taps on the same button
- (fix round 2) the first setup's finish stores the time zone's country (`setupHolCountry`: list empty, `fetched` 0) with `setupDone`, before `setupHolidays` fetches it; `setupHolidays` also fetches for a country kept with no list; swiped away right after finishing, the next start's `refreshHolidays` fetches it
- (fix round 2) the setup's city search clears its results on an empty search and shows a non-English find's English name with the typed one beside it
- an ended invitation: the Connection step says so ("Your invitation has ended, so the link you used no longer works…") and All set reads "invitation ended"
- the setup's day rows and °C/°F are named groups (W2-19-10); a step change says "Step 3 of 7: …" (`announce`) and the keyboard stays on Next / Back (Next when Back is gone) (W2-19-01)
- its controls use data-act (act.js; Round C, wave C2-6, 2026-11-18), registered as setup.js's last statement; no inline handlers: the head and bar (`setupCancelTap`, `setupSkipTap`, `setupBackTap`, `setupNextTap`, which hand the tap itself to `setupCancel` / `setupFinish` / `setupGo` for the double-tap guards), a day (`setupDayTap`, `data-k` the list, `data-s` the day), a switch (`setupSwTap`, `data-k`), the vest (`setupVestPick`, `data-act-change`), Weather (`setupGeoTap`, `setupCityType` on `data-act-input`, `setupCityKey` on `data-act-keydown`, `setupFindTap`, `setupCityTap` with `data-s`, `setupUnitTap` with `data-k`), Connection (`setupLinkKey`, `setupConnectTap`), Today's checklist and the empty Wardrobe (`setupAddTap`, `wemptyImportTap`, `wemptySampleTap`)
- none of them needs `actsFirst`: the setup's tap guard (`setupTapGuard`) is a capture listener on `document`, which holds a `data-act` click back exactly as it held an inline handler back; it is added during the tap that closes the setup (now in `document`'s bubble phase), and a capture listener added there is not run for that same click
- `tests/kitsetup-taps.test.js` (Chromium and WebKit, touch): every control of the setup, the tour, the import sheet, the sample and the checklist sections by real taps, typing and picks; it reads no handler attribute and passes unchanged on the app from before the move

### `kit.js`

- kit.js loads right before setup.js (the restructure S3f, 2026-11-26; it was right after): setup.js draws its Home Screen step, its last step and the sample from kit.js's names (14 forward reads gone); kit.js reads setup.js's `setupShown`, `setupFinish` and `setupSample` when tapped (3 forward reads); its top level only pins, registers handlers and draws About's Home Screen line, and its `window` keydown listener stays after ui.js's and colpick.js's
- getting started (2026-11-14, the owner's ask after the audit's new-user walkthrough), loaded after setup.js; `tests/kit.test.js`
- Safari-only users (2026-11-16, the owner's decision 2 for W1-11-04): the Home Screen step (setup and About's sheet) says once, when not connected, that Safari can clear the app's data if it isn't opened for about a week or runs short of space, and that the Home Screen app and Download backup protect it (`homeRisk`)
- `safariRemind` (from safety.js `safetyStart`, 5 s after start): an iPhone or iPad Safari tab, not connected, `SAFARI_MIN` (10) or more own items, never backed up (no `last_sync`, no `backup_file_at`): a toast with Download backup, at most once every 30 days (`SAFARI_EVERY`, `localStorage.safari_remind`), not over another toast or the setup
- one full-screen sheet (`#kitsheet`, z-index 2000, the page inert while open, Escape closes, ← → step) serves the tour, the close-up guide and the import (`kitOpen`/`kitDraw`/`kitClose`/`kitGo`)
- the sheet takes a history entry or borrows Add's (`kitHist` → back.js `backBorrow('kit')`, kept in `kit.hist`): the phone's Back closes it like ✕, and Add stays under the import sheet (fix round 2, 2026-11-15: Back left the app from the tour and the Home Screen sheet, and closed Add under the import sheet)
- the import sheet's paste field scrolls into view under the header when it is focused (`kitPasteShow`, 350 ms later, for the keyboard)
- the quick tour (`tourOpen(then, lastLabel)`, `TOUR_CARDS`): four cards with drawn pictures: the score, the close-up (white paper along one edge), Shade, patterns; marked seen on close (`localStorage.tour_seen`, `tourSeen`)
- a first-time person who taps "Add my first item" at the setup's end gets the tour between the setup and Add, its last button "Add my first item" (`setupFinish`); Escape or ✕ also goes on to Add
- the tour opened that way says so on its first card (`kit.lead`, `.kit-lead`: "First, four quick cards … ✕ skips straight there"; W2-14-07)
- a tap on the tour's Next or Back within `KIT_TAP_MS` (350) of the last is dropped as a double tap's second (`kitGo`, real clicks only; W2-14-06: a double tap skipped the close-up card); tests wait past it
- Add to Home Screen names the browser it is open in (2026-11-16, a tester in Chrome on an iPhone was shown Safari's steps): `homeBrowser()` (kit.js: CriOS Chrome, FxiOS Firefox, EdgiOS Edge, else Safari; `window.DW_BROWSER` in tests) picks step 1 (`homeShareStep`: Chrome's Share at the right end of its address bar, else its ⋯ menu; Firefox and Edge their menu's Share; Safari as before) and step 2's hint (Edit Actions, Safari's View More); `homeNote`, Settings → Connection's line (account.js) and the reminder line (remind.js, `homeShareShort`) say the browser's name; every iOS browser draws with Safari's engine and a Home Screen app added from any of them keeps its own storage; `tests/homescreen.test.js`
- the tour is offered again by "Take the 1-minute tour" on the setup's last step and Today's first-outfit checklist (`kitStartRow`), and Settings → About → "Take the tour" (`#tour-again`)
- the close-up guide (`kitCloseupGuide`, `GUIDE_CARD`): fill the screen with cloth, plain white paper along one edge out of the dashed middle (where the colour is measured), the same light on both, the paper white not blown out, tap the paper; opened from the Add card's close-up step ("Tips for a good close-up", `.texguide` in `texSlot`) and the tour's close-up card; its pictures draw the paper as a straight band
- the Add card's close-up step itself says "Lay white paper along one edge and tap it on the screen to set the exposure." (`.texpaper` in add.js `texSlot`, the owner's ask), so a returning user sees it without opening the guide
- Undo after Confirm (`kitSaved`, called at the end of add.js `save`): "Saved: <name>." with Undo for `KIT_UNDO_MS` (8 s); cards saved within 1.5 s of each other are one Undo ("Saved 2 items")
- How many (add.js `qtyRow`/`stepQty`/`qtyOf`, 2026-11-14, the owner: "tap a plus or minus for the amount before I add", shirts and socks only, `QTY_TYPES`): a "How many" stepper above Confirm, 1 to `QTY_MAX` (12), 44 px buttons, changed in place (no redraw); Confirm reads "Confirm 3" and `save` makes that many items, each its own id (deep copies of the first: same tags, photo and close-up; the server keeps a photo once by its hash), so the planner and laundry count them apart; the toast "Saved 3: White." and its Undo take them all back (`kitSaved(it,q,i,copies)`); another type ignores a number set before; `tests/addcards.test.js`
- `kitQuiet` silences Undo while it is set (nothing sets it since the import and the sample stopped going through `save`)
- `kitUnsave` deletes the items (tombstones, as Delete) and puts their cards back on Add in their places; `q.raw` and the canvases were already dropped by save, so white balance can't be toggled again on a restored card
- Duplicate (`kitDuplicate`, `#iv-dup` in itemsheet.js): a deep copy with a new id and `added` now, without `paused`, `order`, `arrives`, `away`, `cleaned`, `sample` (`KIT_NO_COPY`); opens the copy
- Duplicate is its own row (`.ivdup`) above Delete, which stays alone at the very end of the item sheet (`tests/wardrobetap.test.js`, `tests/uipages.test.js`)
- import from a spreadsheet (`kitImportOpen`: Add → "Import from a spreadsheet" `#add-import`, and the empty Wardrobe card): a CSV/TSV file or pasted rows (`kitSplit`: tab, ; or , from the first row, quotes)
- the first row names the columns in any order (`KIT_COLS`: Type and Colour required; second/third colour, pattern, name, brand, material, notes, vest, cuffs, kind, style, leather, season)
- words are matched to the app's lists (`KIT_TYPE_WORDS`; `kitColour`: colour names as shown, the owner's own colours by name; `kitPattern` with synonyms)
- a preview row per line says what was left out and why (left-out rows first; "Type is empty", "Colour is empty", an unknown type lists what the app plans, an unknown colour lists the type's colours); nothing is saved until "Add N items"; "Download a template" (`KIT_TEMPLATE`, a `data:` link: a blob revoked a second later can fail on an iPhone)
- many items at once are written in ONE IndexedDB transaction (`kitTx`, `kitStore`, `kitDelMany` with their tombstones and day records; 2026-11-15, the new-user audit): the import, the sample, their Undo and removal store all or nothing on a kill, and a double tap is ignored (`kitImpBusy`, `kitSampling`); items are built as add.js `saveCard` builds a hand-added card (`kitItem`), not through `save`; a write that throws part-way aborts the transaction
- import words: plurals and everyday type names (`kitType`: Blazers, Watches, Dress shirts), everyday colours through core.js `COL_ALIAS` and "<colour> grey" (`kitColour`, said as "read as …"), a colour the type isn't offered in moved by `fitCol` (said), materials matched to `MATERIALS_BY` in any case and shoes'/belts' Material as their leather (`kitMaterial`), a "Kind" column, Striped/Check/Repp/Bengal stripe as patterns
- import materials (2026-11-15, the materials audit): another word for a listed material is read as it (core.js `matAlias`: Merino, Fresco, Broadcloth, Lisle, Wool-linen, Wool-silk, Camel, Nylon/spandex…, said "read as"); a shirt's weave in the Material column (oxford, twill, end-on-end…, `SHIRT_WEAVE_MAT`) becomes its pattern when the row's is plain (said), else is left out (said); any other word is kept as typed (Other)
- shoes' and belts' leather from the Material or Leather column (`kitLeather`): "Pebble grain" / "Scotch grain" with a space are the grains (they fell through to "leather" and saved as calf), Nubuck is nubuck where the style takes it, else suede (said), "braided" a braided belt, "leather"/"calf" calf
- import files are decoded by `kitDecode` (UTF-8, UTF-16 by its mark, else Windows-1252); an empty file says so
- import preview rows have a tick (`kitImpTick`); a row matching a piece already in the wardrobe (type, colours, pattern, name: `kitDupKey`) starts unticked as "already in your wardrobe" (`kitDupMark`); "Add N items" shows "Adding k of n…" over 40 rows, Cancel and ✕ wait for it
- the import's Undo lasts `KIT_IMP_UNDO_MS` (20 s); afterwards the import sheet offers "Take them out" for the last import (`KIT_IMP_LAST` in localStorage, `kitUnimport`)
- the sample wardrobe (`kitSampleLoad`, `KIT_SAMPLE`: 24 pieces, three suits, two blazers, two trousers, five shirts, five ties, shoes, belts, socks, a watch): saved with `item.sample`, asks first when the wardrobe has own pieces and no sample, jumps to the next workday (`goToday`); offered on the setup's last step, Today's checklist and the empty Wardrobe
- the sample follows the dress code (`kitSampleCards`: no suits/blazers without jackets, then `KIT_SAMPLE_NOJKT` two more trousers; no ties without ties); a tap adds only the pieces missing (`kitSampleSig`: type, colours, pattern, cuffs), so a partial sample from before is completed, never doubled
- the sample from the setup's last step finishes the setup first (`kitSampleDone` → `setupFinish(false)`: holidays, the no-vest default)
- a sample piece the owner changed (`kitEdited`: `item.sampleMine`, set by an edit in the item sheet) or kept ("Keep it as mine" in the item sheet, `kitKeepSample`, `kitSampleNote`) is his; the grid tags the others "Sample" (`kitSampleTag`, wardrobe.js); item pictures leave sample pieces out (photos.js `missingItemPics`)
- the Wardrobe grid's eyebrow names chinos and jeans ("Chinos"), not "Trousers"
