- Add to Home Screen (2026-11-14, the owner: show a new iPhone user how to add it as a web app): on an iPhone or iPad in a browser tab (`homeNeeded` in kit.js: `homeIOS` and not `homeStandalone`; `window.DW_IOS` / `DW_STANDALONE` for tests) the setup's steps are worked out when it opens (`SETUP_STEPS = setupStepsNow()`, from `SETUP_BASE`) with a Home Screen step right after Welcome (`homeSteps(true)`): the reason (full screen, the only way iOS lets it send the morning reminder), three taps with the Share and Add icons (Share at the bottom of Safari, at the top on an iPad), and "do this before you set anything up": the Home Screen app keeps its own storage, apart from Safari's; Next carries on in Safari; Settings → About's "Add to Home Screen" (`#home-again`, `homeOpen`, the same instructions as a kit sheet) shows only there (`homeAbout`, set once at start; `#home-again[hidden]{display:none}`: the Settings buttons' display rule outranked the attribute); elsewhere the setup is as before (6 steps); `tests/homescreen.test.js`
- Android install (2026-11-14, the owner: "the same thing for android with an install button"): on Android in a browser tab (`homeAndroid`, `window.DW_ANDROID` for tests) the setup's step after Welcome is "Install the app" (`homeStepsAndroid`; `SETUP_TITLES.home` is a getter): the browser's own offer is held (`beforeinstallprompt` → `homeEvt`, its banner prevented) and an Install button (`.home-install`, `homeInstall`: `prompt()` once, then the offer is gone) shows in place of the menu steps (`.home-manual`: ⋮ → Install app / Add to Home screen) only while there is an offer, updated in place (`homeSync`; the offer can come after the step is drawn); `appinstalled` toasts; no "set up first" warning (the installed app shares the browser's storage); About's button reads "Install the app" (`homeLabel`); `[hidden]` on them needs `display:none!important` (the setup's button rule outranks it); `tests/homescreen.test.js` fakes the offer
- (audit 2026-11-15, H12) one Home Screen explanation (`homeSteps`, iPhone): ⋯ then Share (Share at the top right on an iPad), Add to Home Screen (or View More), Open as Web App on, Add, open it; a connected tester gets "Copy my invitation link" (`homeCopy`, `homeTester`) and is told to paste it at the Home Screen app's Connection step; the note (`homeNote`) says what applies: connected, not connected with a wardrobe (it stays in Safari: Download backup / Restore file), or not connected and empty (set it up there)
- account.js's `homeScreenGuide` opens that sheet (never over the setup); About's buttons call `tourAgain`/`homeAgain`, which go back to Settings → About on close
- the tour's score card speaks of shirts and trousers without ties or jackets (`kitWears`)
- Today shows "You're trying a sample wardrobe" (`kitSampleBanner`, `#kit-sample`) with Add my own and Remove the sample (`kitSampleRemove`: asks, says "Removing the sample…" at once, deletes every sample piece he didn't change and takes them out of every day record that names one (`kitSampleDay`), in one transaction, releases locks); `tests/kit.test.js`
- `kitSampleDay` (re-audit 2026-11-15, N2: cutting a record to its occasion let history.js `recordDays` refill the past day from today's plan: "Didn't" days became worn, a suit bought later was worn before it came): a past day (or one marked worn) keeps everything (worn, `edited`, occasion, his own pieces) with the sample's slots null and marked in `rec.gone` (planner.js `frozenOutfit` rates it without them, as after a Delete)
- a past day that wore only sample pieces becomes `rec.noOutfit` (no `ids`): `recordDays` leaves it, `planSim` skips it (no outfit, no rest or variety from it), Insights doesn't count it, Today says "No outfit recorded: this day was dressed from the sample wardrobe, which you removed." (`#nooutfit`)
- today and coming days lose their sample pieces, lock and picks (`force`) and are planned again; `tests/sample.test.js`
- the sample is never backed up (re-audit 2026-11-15, N7: loaded while connected it went into the person's real wardrobe and showed on his other phones): kit.js `kitShareFilter` (called by sync.js) leaves out of every upload (small sync and whole backup) the sample pieces he hasn't made his (`kitEdited`); this phone's copy and the merge are unchanged
- a day record naming one goes up with the sample's pieces taken out (fix round 3, W1-3-02/W1-3-03: held back whole, a "Didn't" or a swap never reached the backup and a phone joining was told he hadn't opened the app): `kitShareFilter` sends `kitSampleDay(rec, off, tomorrow)` (today counted as past, so his pieces stay, the sample's slots null and in `rec.gone`; a coming day loses the sample's lock and picks) with the record's own stamp, only when that still holds something of his (`kitDayOwn`: his own pieces, a pick, a lock, an occasion, a kind, a skip, a piece off, a trip)
- a day made only from the sample (`kitDayOwn` false) and one stamped `KIT_YIELD` never go up: they would replace the account's own record of that day; a whole-backup save (PUT replaces the backup) sends the backup's own copy of a day it leaves out (`kitShareFilter(st, remote.data, join)`)
- except a "Didn't" / "Wore" or a correction (`worn` set, `edited`) made on such a day after this phone first synced with the account (`syncJoinAt`: localStorage `sync_join` {k: the code's last 6 characters, at}, set by `syncOnce` before its first request, `syncJoinMark`; at 1 when the phone already synced with this server, `syncMark`: in the account all along): it goes up with no outfit (`noOutfit`, `worn` kept), so a phone updated from 15 Nov with the sample in its backup gets its marks to the other phones (W1-3-02), while a phone that tried the sample before joining sends none of its sample days (W1-6-04)
- a day of sample pieces alone recorded after joining, with no mark of his, goes up with no outfit and the stamp `KIT_YIELD` (W1-3-03: a phone joining later recorded it from its own plan and said "you didn't open the app"): the backup takes it only where it has no record of the day or only the app's lock built on nothing (`dayWins`, base 0), never over a real record; the record's own stamp decides whether it is sent (`kitOrig`, `kitSentStamp` in `syncLite`'s filter)
- today and coming days (and those yielding copies) go up once per change of what he decided on them (`kitSdSig`: worn, edited, noOutfit, picks, occasion, kind, skip, off, trip, vest), not for the app's own locks and recordings: a phone with the sample and one without re-locked each other's copy at every sync (lab: a write every other background); `localStorage.sync_sd` {date: sig} is written after a save went through (`kitShareDone`, from sync.js), kept `KIT_SD_DAYS` (60)
- Remove the sample gives a day it leaves with nothing of his the stamp `KIT_YIELD` (1; `kitDelMany` keeps it) instead of a new one, so the account's record of that day made on another phone wins the next merge (fix round 3, W1-6-04, Critical: a phone that tried the sample before joining stamped its sample days new and wiped the other phone's real days, past, today and locked); connected, it syncs (up to `KIT_RM_SYNC` 8 s) before Today is drawn again, so today isn't recorded from the plan over the account's record; a day with his pieces takes a new stamp as before
- a sample piece is his (`kitEdited`) only once he edits it in the item sheet: app.js `put` calls `kitUserEdit`, which sets `item.sampleMine` while the item sheet is open on that item (`editing`); a new stamp alone (a roll back, a start-up fix-up such as `migrateVests`, `checkArrivals`, `texSwatches`) no longer makes it his (re-audit 2026-11-15, Fixer 4's review: it was any stamp 3 s after `added`, so such a piece was uploaded and kept on Remove the sample)
- taking a sample piece out of rotation (or back) doesn't make it his: itemsheet.js `togglePause` sets `kitNoMine` around its `put` (re-audit 2026-11-15: paused sample shoes stayed after Remove the sample)
- the sample pieces the earlier rule counted as his stay his (fix round 3, W1-3-01: after the update they were "Sample" again and Remove the sample deleted them with their worn days): `kitSampleMigrate` (start.js, `whenPulled`, once per phone: `localStorage.kit_mine`) makes a sample piece added before `KIT_MINE_CUT` (2026-11-15 12:00 UTC, when the rule changed) and stamped more than `KIT_EDIT_MS` after `added`/`sampleBase` (`kitOldMine`, the old rule) his as Keep it as mine does (no `sample`, a new stamp, so it goes up), with a toast
- the sample banner shows only while a sample piece he hasn't made his is there (`kitHasSample`; a phone joining got "You're trying a sample wardrobe" for his own pieces)
- Remove the sample's toast has Undo (`kitSampleUndo`, `kitRmUndo`, 8 s): the pieces come back as they were with a new stamp (newer than their deletion marks), and each day still as Remove left it (`wrote`) goes back as it was, with its old stamp when it had `KIT_YIELD`; a day the account's record replaced meanwhile keeps that; the released locks stay released; `tests/sample.test.js`
- `kitSampleLoad` first takes in items another tab of the app stored (`kitAdopt`, from the database): a stale second tab loaded a second sample (48 pieces, on the server too)
- the setup's "Try a sample wardrobe" (setup.js `setupSample`) finishes the setup only once the sample is stored (`kitSampleDone`); finished first, a kill in between left the setup done with no sample
- import: a row matching only sample pieces is not "already in your wardrobe" (`kitDupMark` skips sample pieces he hasn't made his; re-audit 2026-11-15, N5: his rows were left unticked and gone after Remove the sample)
- import words (re-audit 2026-11-15): own colours and patterns by name through `CUSTOM_COL`/`CUSTOM_PAT` (`Object.entries` on the colours list took its index: "0 isn't one of the tie colours"); a watch's or cufflinks' colour that is a metal sets the metal (`kitMetal`: gold, rose gold → gold, silver/steel, gunmetal → 'other'), and a Metal / Buckle column is read for watches, belts and cufflinks
- import: several colours in one cell ("light blue, white", "Blue/white", "navy & burgundy & white") fill empty second and third colour columns; `kitType` finds a known word among others ("Shoes - Oxford", "Linen trouser"; last word first), plus Trouser, Oxford shirt, Suit jacket (a suit); Mauve and Fuchsia/Magenta are read
- the kit sheet's ← → step only cards: not in the import sheet (it has none: → closed it and lost the pasted rows) nor while typing in a field
- the import is a bottom sheet (2026-11-15, add-page-mockups): `kitImportOpen` sets `#kitsheet.kbs` (`kitBs`: top `--ivtop`, from 600px `max(4vh, var(--ivtop))`, at most 640px wide, rounded top, `kbsUp` slide-in), `#kit-backdrop` (a tap closes it as ✕), the grabber `.kit-grab` and the table icon (`KIT_TABLE`, `.kit-hic`) in its header, a swipe down closes it (`kitImportSwipe` → add.js `sheetSwipe`, not while writing); closing slides it away (`kitBsAway`, `.going`, emptied 320 ms later, inert meanwhile); the tour and guides stay full screen (`kitOpen` → `kitBs(false)`); its content is unchanged
- "Take them out" (`kitLastImp`) leaves imported pieces changed since the import (stamped over `KIT_EDIT_MS` after it) and says so, and is offered for `KIT_IMP_KEEP_MS` (7 days), then forgotten
- import, fix round 3 (2026-11-16, the third audit; `tests/kit.test.js`): each row is read by `kitRow(cells, line)` → `{line, ok, card, probs, qty, given}`; `given` holds the fields the row itself says (an update changes only those)
- import: a Description / Details / Item column (`KIT_COLS.desc`; "Item" is the type only when no other column names it), Notes, the name and the type cell give the pattern (`kitPatWords`), the trousers' kind, the shoes' style (monk → derby, penny → loafer, chelsea → boot), a three-piece suit, French cuffs and a material when their own columns are empty (W2-15-01: "charcoal pinstripe", "knit", "chinos" came in plain)
- import: a description's words nothing was read from (`kitLeft`) become the name ("Penny loafers", "Brogues"), or go to the notes when the row has a name; the preview names the description column read and the columns not used (`.kit-cols`: "Not used: Size.")
- import: the column names may be in any of the first 6 rows (a title above them is skipped, said); headers also "Item type", "Clothing", "Piece", "Colours" (`kitHead`, `kitCols`); without column names the text is read as a list, one piece per line ("Navy suit", "White shirt x3": `kitFree`, `kitReadList`) when at least half its lines name a type and a colour (W2-15-05)
- import: a Qty column, or "x3"/"3x" in the description, name or type, makes that many pieces, at most `KIT_QTY_MAX` (12; a row's `qty`); Qty 0 leaves the row out (W2-15-06)
- import: `kitSplit` gives each row its place in the text (`r.ln`, blank rows counted), so "Row N" is the row pasted (W2-15-04); the delimiter is taken from the first 6 lines
- import colours (`kitColour`): "lt."/"dk"/"med" and short forms (brn, wht, nvy, `KIT_COL_ABBR`), "sky", one-typo names ("burgandy", `kitNear`), Arabic color words with فاتح/غامق (`KIT_AR_COL`); a colour of which only the last word was understood says so (`part`: "only “grey” of “heather grey” was understood"); the app's own names, hyphen included, are exact (W2-15-03, W2-15-07, W2-18-04)
- import colours: the app's own color names come before the owner's own colours (own colours named "Navy" or "Gray" on the 14 Nov version took "Suit, Navy" and every "Grey" row, W1-2-02); own colours still come before everyday names (`COL_ALIAS`)
- import types: one-typo type words ("Shrit", `kitTypeNear`, words of 5+ letters), Brogues, Wingtips, Monk straps, Moccasins, Khakis, and Arabic type words (kitNorm drops the hamza on alif and turns ة into ه, Arabic-Indic digits into 0-9); an unknown type asks for English when it is in Arabic, and a row with an unknown type and colour says both (W2-16-06, W2-15-07)
- import: name, brand, material and notes are cut to what is kept (`WARD_MAX`) in the preview's card, said ("name cut to 60 characters", W2-12-05)
- import re-run (the owner's decision 5, W2-15-02): `kitDupMark` first matches rows to pieces already there (`kitDupKey`, a row of 3 with 1 there adds 2), then a row of the same type and colours (main and second) as one of his pieces, with another pattern, name or details (`kitDiff` over `KIT_UPD`), is offered as an update of that piece (`x.upd`, `x.diff`, li `.upd`: "updates your navy tie: pattern solid → knit"); a row that says nothing new about such a piece is that piece; each piece is matched once (name, then brand, then pattern break ties); sample pieces he hasn't made his never match
- import re-run: the button says "Update 7 pieces" / "Add 3, update 7" (`kitAdds`, `kitUpds`); "Add as new" on a row (`kitAsNew`) adds it instead; `kitImportGo` writes the new pieces and the updated copies (`kitApply`) in one transaction (`kitStore(list, prog, upd)`), keeping each piece's id (and so its history); Undo puts the old copies back (`kitUnsave({its, qs, olds})` → `kitDelMany(ids, olds)`); "Take them out" lists only the added pieces
- a picked file that can't be read (an iCloud file not downloaded) says "This file couldn’t be read…" instead of throwing (`kitImportFile`, W2-20-01)
- the 14 Nov import saved a row naming an own colour as its index in `settings.custom.colors` ("0", "1"): `kitFixOldImports` (start.js, after the first pull, `whenPulled`) turns such a colour into the one its own colour's name means today through `kitColour` ("Gray" → gray, "Navy" → navy, "Mocha" → the own Mocha), fitted to the type, stamped so it syncs; nothing to do once no colour is a number (W1-2-01)

- "Take them out" (`kitUnimport`) syncs first when connected (fix round 3, W1-6-05), so a piece renamed on another phone counts as changed since and stays; offline it judges this phone's copy
- its controls use data-act (act.js; Round C, wave C2-6, 2026-11-18), registered as kit.js's last statement; no inline handlers: the sheet (`kitShut` for ✕ and Cancel, `kitBackTap`, `kitNextTap`, `kitGuideTap`), the import (`kitUnimportTap`, `kitTemplateTap`, `kitFilePick` on `data-act-change`, `kitPasteFocus` on `data-act-focus`, `kitPasteType` on `data-act-input`, `kitImpTap`, a preview row's `kitTickPick` and `kitAsNewTap` with `data-k`, its place in `kitImp.rows`), the sample and the ways in (`kitKeepTap` with `data-id`, `kitAddOwnTap`, `kitSampleRemoveTap`, `kitTourTap`, `kitSampleTap`, and `kitSetupSampleTap` on the setup's last step, which hands the tap to `setupSample`), the Home Screen step (`homeInstallTap`, `homeCopyTap`)
- `kitGo` still reads the tap from `window.event` (a trusted click with `detail`): inside act.js's listener that is the same click
- `kitStartRow` writes both names out in full (`' data-act="kitSetupSampleTap"'` or `' data-act="kitSampleTap"'`): `tests/acts.test.js` refuses a handler name made up at run time
### `calendar.js`

- calendar import (2026-11-17, the owner's ask: "share a calendar subscription link once, and the app sets up occasion days, trips and days off"), loaded after kit.js; `tests/calendar.test.js` (fixtures `tests/fixtures/calendar/*.ics`, made up; no real calendar is ever fetched)
- Settings → Calendar (`#sc-calendar`, after Days off & occasions, `SET_CATS` 'calendar', for everyone): drawn by `renderCalUI` (a `settingsPart`) into `#cal-box`; summary `calSummary` ("Not connected", "3 new events to check", "Connected · up to date")
- not connected to a calendar: a link field (`#cal-link`, webcal:// or https://) and Connect (`calConnect`: reads it first, saves it only when the server read it); without a server connection Connect is off with "Reading a calendar needs a connection: Connect in ⚙ Settings first."
- connected: "Connected" and the link masked to its host (`calMask`: "p52-caldav.icloud.com/…"; the full link is never shown again), the status line `calStatus` ("Last read 7:02 PM · 3 events used"), Read now (`calRefresh`), Disconnect (`calDisconnect`, asks; days and occasions already set stay)
- the link is `settings.calendar` {url, at} (synced like any setting; core.js `SET_CLEAN.calendar` keeps only a webcal/https link up to 2048 characters); kept and dismissed events are `settings.calSeen` {id: 'k'|'x'} (merge.js `SET_SPLIT` 'obj': two phones' choices merge entry by entry)
- the last answer is kept on this phone only: `localStorage.cal_last` {t, url: the masked host, from, to, events, err} (in `ABOUT_KEYS`); it never holds the link
- read again by itself at start-up (`whenPulled`) and on coming back (`visibilitychange`) at most every `CAL_EVERY` (3 h): `calAuto` → `calSync`
- the keyword rules are data (`CAL_RULES`, first match wins): funeral/memorial → funeral; wedding/nikah → wedding; gala, black tie, formal, ceremony, interview, awards, graduation, reception → formal day; casual Friday/Thursday, dress down → casual day; PTO, leave, vacation, holiday, OOO, out of office, day off, off (all-day only; not kick-off, sign-off, offsite…) → days off; trip, travel, flight, conference, offsite, summit… → a trip when it lasts 2+ days or is a flight
- a 2+ day event whose place names another city than the weather location (`calHome`; not online, Teams, Zoom, a room) is a trip too; an outbound flight and the next flight within 21 days make one trip (`calFound`)
- the review list (`#cal-review`, "Found N events"): each row the title, dates and place and what keeping it does ("→ Wedding day"), ✓ (`calKeep`) and ✕ (`calDismiss`); kept and dismissed rows below with "Used · Undo" / "Dismissed · Undo" (`calUndoSeen`: offered again; what was set stays)
- an occasion only on a workday (`calPlan`: the planner plans no other day): a Saturday wedding reads "Saturday isn't a workday: no outfit to plan" and can only be dismissed
- an occasion on a workday of the week that is a day off gives the real reason (`calOffWhy`, 2026-11-18): "a day off (Christmas Day): no outfit to plan" for a holiday, "a day off (your day off): …" for the person's own day off (it wins, as in `offDays`); `tests/calendar.test.js` with a made-up holiday and day off
- ✓ goes through the app's own functions: an occasion `setOccasion(occ, key(d))` for each workday of the event (at most 7; locks released as for a day set by hand), days off settings.js `dayOffAdd(from, to, title)` (split out of `addDayOff`), a trip pack.js `packSetTrip(a, b)` (the Packing list's dates, no limits) with a toast "Packing list set for …" and Open
- Today (`calBanner(sel)`, prepended to the stage in `renderToday`): on the home day "N new calendar events" with Review (opens Settings → Calendar) while kept-able matches are unseen; on a day of a kept trip "Trip: <title> · Packing list"
- nothing is applied by itself: new matches only raise the banner
- the walkthrough (`calGuide('icloud'|'google'|'outlook')`, `CAL_GUIDES`): kit.js cards (`kitOpen`) with drawn mock screens (`calScreen`; no screenshots of other companies' apps), closing goes back to Settings → Calendar; iPhone: a calendar of its own, Calendars → ⓘ → Public Calendar → Share Link… → Copy (it makes that calendar public by link); Google: calendar.google.com on the web → ⚙ Settings → the calendar → Integrate calendar → Secret address in iCal format; Outlook on the web: ⚙ → Calendar → Shared calendars → Publish a calendar → the ICS link; each says the menu may be named slightly differently; the last card is "Paste it here"
- screenshots of the cards at 390 px light and dark: `CAL_SHOTS=<folder> node tests/run.js calendar` (2026-11-17 set: `C:\code\audit-2026-11-13\calendar\shots\`)
- the phone reads the calendar, the server only relays it (2026-11-25, the owner's design for the review's R6 N2: POST /calendar's parse took about 12 ms of CPU on a 1.4 MB calendar, over the free plan's 10 ms, and past 6 MB it cut the coming events and occasions silently): `calFeed` asks `POST /calendar/feed` (worker/calendar.js) for the feed's own bytes and cal-worker.js parses them with calparse.js, the parser POST /calendar uses, so the events are the server's byte for byte
- every event of the 60 days is kept in `cal_last`, whatever the feed's size (the 300 cap went with the server's parse); `calWin()` is the window: `isoKey(todayDate())` and 60 days in the phone's zone
- cal-worker.js is a module worker (`calWorker`, `{type:'module'}`); the feed goes to it piece by piece as it arrives (`begin`, `part`, then `read` with `parts`); where no Worker can start the page parses it itself (`calHere`, `import('./calparse.js')`), and when the Worker fails with the feed it is asked for again and read on the page
- the last good copy is kept by the worker in Cache Storage `dw-keep-cal` (one entry, named by a hash of the link, never the link; its etag, last-modified and ticket in the entry's headers); a read sends them (`etag`, `lm`, `tk`), an unchanged calendar answers 204 and the kept copy is read for today's window; Delete my data clears it with the other `dw-keep-*` caches, Disconnect with `{op:'forget'}`
- a calendar whose host sends neither an ETag nor a Last-Modified (or that couldn't be kept) comes whole on every read, so `cal_last.whole` is set and it is read by itself at most every `CAL_EVERY_WHOLE` (12 h, else `CAL_EVERY` 3 h): at most 2.8 MB a day for a 1.4 MB calendar, 12 MB for 6 MB; Read now still reads it; whether Google's secret iCal address sends either isn't documented (2026-11-25)
- a read that fails (offline, the server down, the link gone) reads the kept copy for today's window (`calKept`) and keeps the error for the Settings line, as before
- a server from before the relay (404/405 on /calendar/feed) is asked through POST /calendar as before (`calFetch`)
- measured (`tests/calfeed.test.js`, 2026-11-25): a 6 MB calendar connects in about 0.2 s on Chromium and 0.3 s on WebKit, the longest frame gap once its answer has come 17 ms and 21 ms; 24 MB: 0.5 s and 0.65 s, gaps 17 ms and 24 ms (Playwright's WebKit hands a mocked body over on the page's thread before fetch() resolves: 51 ms at 6 MB, 173 ms at 24 MB, which a phone's network process doesn't)
- the privacy line under Calendar now says "The server fetches the calendar when the app asks and keeps nothing; this phone reads it and uses only dates, titles and places." (it said the server sends back only dates, titles and places)
- `tests/calfeed.test.js`: the relay (bytes, CPU at 0.05, 1.4, 6 and 24 MB, 204, the limits, the ticket, no KV), the shared parser against main's frozen copy (`tests/fixtures/calendar/main-calendar.js`) and POST /calendar's answers, and the app on both engines (Connect at 6 MB, an unchanged day, the next day, a change, offline, an older server, no Worker); made-up feeds from `tests/calfeeds.js`
- `tests/replay/scripts/CR-6-20.js` (port 9230): the app before this change against the server before and after it gives the same events, words, review list and Settings line for the fixture, 1.4 MB and 6 MB calendars; today's app keeps every event the older one had (and the 6 MB calendar's occasions); the lab's `POST /__mock/cal` serves a made-up calendar

### `remind.js`

- morning reminder (after account.js): `#remind-box` (`.conn-only`), `remindOn`/`remindOff`, `remindTime`; per phone in `localStorage.remind`
- switching on asks permission, subscribes and posts the time, time zone and coming workdays
- `remindLines` (next 14 workdays, `piecesLine`) are kept by `remindKeep` in `dw-keep-remind` as `remind.json` for sw.js
- `remindSoon` rewrites them and tells the server only when dates or time change, or once a day (`remind_sent`)
- going to the background writes the kept lines at once (`remindHidden`, local, no request; fix round A, CR-4-09): iOS suspends a page within seconds, so a swap, rename or deletion just before closing missed the 3 s refresh; a sync that brings changes calls `remindSoon` too (start.js `onSyncedData`)
- a phone already told asks the server about twice a day whether it still has its subscription (`GET /push?endpoint=`, one read, `REMIND_CHECK_MS` 12 h, `remind_sent.chk`) and tells it again when it says off (fix round A, CR-4-10; the server can lose one, CR-4-03); no KV write unless it was lost
- a phone with no push subscription any more (the Home Screen app removed and added again, notifications switched off) switches both reminders off and says "Reminders stopped on this phone. Switch them on again to get them." (`remindLost`); the switch read On while nothing arrived; `tests/remind.test.js`
- on iPhone Safari it asks to add to the Home Screen first (Web Push only there, iOS 16.4+)
- sw.js: `push` shows "Today’s outfit" with today's line, `notificationclick` opens the app; keep the title UTF-8 (it was once saved garbled)

### `about.js`

- Settings → About (`SET_CATS` ends with `about`, `#sc-about`, `renderAbout`): version and updates, feedback, deleting one's data, the privacy note; loaded last
- `appVersion()`: the `CACHE` number in the cached `sw.js` (`swVersionOf`, `aboutReadVersion`), so the version running, not the newest
- `checkForUpdate` (start-up, foreground after `ABOUT_FRONT_MS`, every `ABOUT_CHECK_MS`, the button): `aboutLatest` fetches `sw.js` with `cache:'no-store'`; a higher number downloads it and offers Update once per version (`aboutToasted`, `aboutShow`); never reloads by itself
- `updateNow()`: asks first only if a photo on Add is still being read (below), sends the waiting worker `{type:'skipWaiting'}` and reloads on `controllerchange`; gives up after `ABOUT_INSTALL_MS` (45 s) without reloading
- `aboutAdopt()`: a waiting worker of the version already running takes over without a reload (`aboutAsk`)
- feedback (`sendFeedback`): `POST /feedback` with `x-app-token` (`aboutConn`); `loadFeedback` lists it in `#fb-list` for the owner only
- delete my data: `askDelete` (`#del-confirm`), `deleteMyData(server)` → `wipeDevice()` → reload
- `wipeDevice()`: deletes the IndexedDB database (`ABOUT_DB`), the `dw-keep-*` caches, online also the service worker and its caches, and the app's own localStorage keys (`ABOUT_KEYS`, `ABOUT_KEY_PREFIXES`); never `localStorage.clear()`, as GitHub Pages sites of one account share an origin
- (CR-7-01) `wipeDevice()` returns whether the database is gone: it first stops the backup (`syncTimer`, waits for `syncRun`) and the connection copy (`connReady`), sets app.js `dbWiping` (no connection may open meanwhile), closes both connections (`db`, the first, and `dbConn`, the reopened one), then deletes the database alone (`aboutDbDelete`) and checks nothing is left (`aboutDbLeft`); it cleared the stores through `db`, closed by iOS, and that error skipped the delete while it said "deleted"
- a delete held up by the app open in another tab can't be called off: it is waited for, `#del-status` says so (`ABOUT_WIPE_WAIT`), and it finishes once that tab lets go
- a delete that fails clears nothing else (no keys, no caches), says so (`ABOUT_WIPE_FAIL`, or `ABOUT_WIPE_FAIL_SERVER` when the server's copy is gone: `del_pending` stays, so the next start finishes) and the app keeps working
- account.js's switch to another account (`wipeDevice` before the new code is stored) keeps the old connection when the clear fails, or the old wardrobe would go into the new account
- (CR-7-06) `loadFeedback` asks `/me` only when account.js doesn't know the role yet (`acctRole()`)
- a new localStorage key must be added to `ABOUT_KEYS`: `tests/about.test.js` fails otherwise
- "Delete everything" sends `DELETE /account` first with the connection removed and sync stopped; a 404 clears the device and says the server copy remains; any other failure restores the connection and deletes nothing
- deleting on the device only also removes the connection, or the next sync would bring the wardrobe back
- the after-restart message goes in `sessionStorage.about_note` (`ABOUT_NOTE`), shown by `aboutStart()`
- the privacy note (`.about-fold`) names everyone who gets something: the server's owner, its hosting company (unnamed) and GitHub Pages, Anthropic, OpenAI, Open-Meteo, Nager.Date / Google
- `tests/about.test.js` mocks `/me`, `/feedback`, `/account`
- `updateNow` asks only while a photo on Add is still being read (`busy`/`multiBusy`); other cards come back after the restart (add.js `addKeep`, called before it), so "they will be lost" is gone (re-audit 2026-11-15)
- Feedback: one send at a time (`fbSending`), and an empty tap within `FB_AGAIN_MS` (3 s) of a send keeps "Thank you" (a double tap read "Write something first."); the day's saves used up (429 `kv-limit`) says to send it again after the reset (`aboutResetAt`), not the backup sentence "kept on this device"
- an ended invitation's Delete card lists this device only, says the server is out of reach and offers only "Delete from this device"; its note doesn't say the server's backup was kept
- a 403 'forbidden token' to a resumed "Delete everything" (`aboutDeleteAccount(keep, true)`), or for a code remembered as a tester's (`aboutKeptTester`: `acct_role.tok` = the kept code), is the deletion having gone through: at start-up the connection is already removed, so `isTester()` said no and a phone killed mid-delete reopened with "nothing was deleted" (re-audit 2026-11-15, agent8 #5)
- refusals are said with the server's `message`, or an older server's sentence in `error`, never a code (`aboutErrText`, `ABOUT_CODES`: "invite off", "forbidden token", "kv-limit" reached testers); Feedback after an ended invitation says so and Send is off (`aboutLive`: `isConnected()`)
- a tester's "Delete everything" says it ends the invitation (the server deletes `invite:<hash>` first, so the link stops working on every phone)
- "Delete everything" keeps `del_pending` ({w, t}) and clears the connection's database copy (`connKeep`) before `DELETE /account` (`aboutDeleteAccount`); a phone killed before the answer finishes it at the next start (`aboutResumeDelete`, from `aboutStart`; app.js `connRestored` restores nothing while it is set), or, the server unreachable, puts the connection back and says so; killed there it used to reopen with the whole wardrobe and "Connected"
- a 403 'forbidden token' to "Delete everything" (the account already deleted on another phone) clears the device and says the server's data was already deleted
- `ABOUT_KEYS` has `tour_seen`, `tex_up`, `photo_up`, `asm_gl` (kept through constants, which `tests/about.test.js` didn't see; `tests/invite.test.js` resolves `const X='key'`) and `del_pending`
- "Delete everything" refused for the day's saves (429 `kv-limit`) says "The server can’t delete anything more today, so nothing was deleted. Try again after <local reset time>…" (`aboutDeleteAccount`, W1-10-01)
- the privacy note doesn't name the server's host (W1-10-06, the round's rule: nothing a tester reads says Cloudflare): "It runs with a cloud hosting company"; `tests/about.test.js`

### `stats.js`

- Insights reaches its parts in other files through bus.js (the restructure S3f, 2026-11-26), where `typeof f==='function'` guards were: sockets `setup-needs` (setup.js `setupNeeds`), `shop-button` (shop.js `shopButton`), `fav-stats-card` (fav.js `favStatsCard`), `cpw-stats-card` (cost.js `cpwStatsCard`), and the topic `sample-asked`, which kit.js pins `kitSampleLoad` for: "Try a sample wardrobe" is offered when `heard('sample-asked')` and its tap announces it
- the stats page (the Insights tab, `#n-stats`, `openStats`/`renderStats`, shown as the `#stats` section by `show('stats')`): `buildStats()` counts what was really worn (`wornDays()`: day records from when the wardrobe existed, not days marked "Not worn") and what is planned for the next six months (`plannedDays()`)
- `wornDays()` leaves out a day with a piece added more than a day after it (a planner warm-up day) only when the app recorded it: a day corrected or marked worn by hand (`edited`, `worn`) counts (the code review CR-3-11)
- sections: 'Your scores', wear counts by type (folded sections build their rows when opened), what costs points, colour balance, pairings and rest, never-worn pieces with a reason, photo tagging (no learning section: removed 2026-11-15)
- 'Your scores' (`#st-trend`): the average score of each of the last `TREND_WEEKS` (8) worn weeks as columns (red when a day had a hard check), with the worn average, the days with a real mistake and the plan's average
- the next six months are planned a week at a time while the phone is idle (`statsPlan`, `STATS_SLICE` 7 days of the shared simulation per `requestIdleCallback`), started 4 s after start-up and by opening the tab (opening Insights used to freeze a phone 1.3 s)
- `show('stats')` calls `statsShow`, which draws the page at once when the months are planned (`statsPlanned`; always with `TEST_PLAN`), else the header and "Planning your next 6 months…" (`.st-wait`) and the rest when done; `renderStats()` called directly still plans at once
- pieces the dress code leaves out are not counted as never worn (`buildStats().unused`)
- pieces are called by the name the user gave, exactly as typed (`statName`, `statName1`, `statName2`), else their description; the tiles say "1 item", "1 outfit", "different outfits planned" (exact pieces; What to buy counts "colour combinations")
- 'Your scores' leaves out the weeks that ended before the wardrobe existed (`planStartDay`), so a new wardrobe shows one or two columns, not six empty ones
- an empty wardrobe's Insights, Packing list and Laundry & cleaning pages offer "Add your first item" and "Try a sample wardrobe" (`emptyAdd`)
- `whyUnused` works out each jacket's best outfit (`allOf`: its `FIT_PANTS` (3) best trousers x its `FIT_SHIRTS` (8) best shirts x every tie) and its best with the one shirt or tie asked about (`withOf`), only for pieces that need a reason, kept in `fitMemo` until the plan cache (`cache`) is replaced, the item count or the settings it reads change (`fitMemoFor`, `fitSettingsSig`); every jacket x trousers x shirt x tie, rebuilt on every `renderStats`, froze Insights 4.5 s at 300 items on opening and on every fold (W2-18-02, fix round 3)
- unfolding a Wear counts section fills only its rows (`statsToggle` → `statRowsHtml` from `statsLast`, the numbers the page was drawn from, while `cache` and the item count are unchanged), not the whole page; `tests/planner-rules.test.js`
- Colour balance lists an owner's colour as its own row by its own name ("Dusty pink"), not the group of the colour it behaves like (W2-17-04)
- 'Your scores' names each week by its first workday on or after the day the wardrobe began (`t.label`): "Nov 13", a Friday before the first day, read as scores from before the app was installed (W2-15-08)
- `whyUnused`: a paused item was taken out of rotation; an item on the way says so, with its arrival date if there is one; a piece away says "It's at the cleaner's" / "It's in the laundry", with the date it joins the outfits again; a dress-code exclusion says so ("Your dress code leaves out ties…")
- `whyUnused` never says a suiting tie, shirt or pair of trousers "comes up later than six months" (it can't know that); it says the others suit a little better or have rested longer, so it isn't in the next six months' plan
- Wear counts: a type the dress code leaves out (`dressOut`: blazers and suits with Jackets off) says "left out by your dress code", never "N never worn" (re-audit 2026-11-15); `tests/stats.test.js`
- `plannedDays()` takes What to buy's days (`statsDays` = analysis.js `horizonKeys(today, SHOP_WEEKS)`: the workdays of 26 weeks from tomorrow), so Insights' "outfits planned" and What to buy's "workdays" are the same number (they were 122 and 126); `statsMonths` reaches the horizon's last day
- a wardrobe that can't dress a day yet (`statsShort`: setup.js `setupNeeds` rows not ticked, as Today's checklist) gets no plan numbers: the tiles keep items and days worn, a "Not a full outfit yet" card (`#st-short`) names what is missing, and Never worn, Small deductions, Colour balance, Pairings and the plan's average are left out (it said "121 outfits planned … 50.0 average" for a suit and a shirt)
- its controls use data-act (act.js; C2-8, 2026-11-22), registered as stats.js's last statement: `emptyAddTap` and `emptySampleTap` (`emptyAdd()`'s two buttons, and "Add an item"), `statsFold` (a Wear counts section, `data-act-toggle`), `statsDayTap` (a lowest-scoring day's link, `data-t` the day's time; it calls the link's jump off)
- the dependency ledger gained stats.js -> month.js (`openDay`: the call was text in the markup before, which the ledger can't see)

### `pack.js`

- the packing list page (Settings → Packing list `#pack-btn`; the month on Today has no link to it, the owner took that button out): the `#pack` section, shown by `show('pack')` through `packShow(t)` (called at the end of `show()`), `openPack`/`renderPack`
- the trip (`packTrip()`, `localStorage.pack_trip` `{a, b, j, s}`: first and last day as `YYYY-MM-DD`, most jackets and pairs of shoes, 0 = no limit) defaults to the workdays of the week starting on the user's next first workday (`packDefault`, `packWeekStart`: a workday whose day before is not one; Sunday for Sunday-Thursday, Monday for Monday-Friday or when there is no such day)
- a stored trip that is over makes way for the default; at most `PACK_MAX` (31) days (`packSetDate`)
- `packDays(t, plan)`: every day of the range with its outfit from `planMonth` (or from a plan passed in), `o` null on a day off or a day with nothing planned; the page lists each with the month's stack drawing (`stackSVG`) and `shortTop`/`shortRest`
- `packList(days, wear)` → `{groups, worn, counts, total}`: every distinct piece once with its days (`n`), grouped by type in `TYPES` order (`PACK_TITLE`); a suit's `o.bot` is the suit itself and is not counted again; a suit worn with its vest says so
- one belt per shoe colour worn (`leatherGroup` of the shoes): the belt planned most often with that colour, so two brown belts planned on different days are one line
- what the wardrobe lacks is a line without an item (keys `x-belt-<group>`, `x-links`, `x-coat`): a belt for shoes with none that matches, cufflinks for a French-cuff shirt, a coat on a cold or wet day
- the count line (`packCount`, `PACK_COUNTED`: suits, blazers, dress pants, shirts, ties, shoes, socks; nouns in `PACK_NOUN`)
- ticks and travel wear are kept per trip on the device (`localStorage.pack_marks`: `{'<a>_<b>': {t:[line keys], w:[item ids], at}}`, the newest `PACK_TRIPS` 8; `packMark`/`packSetMark`, `packTick`, `packWear`)
- a piece marked Wear moves to "Wearing on the way" and is not counted; `packWearDay` marks the first outfit day's whole outfit (`packWornIds`: its belt is the list's belt for that shoe colour)
- pack lighter (`packLight(t, base)`, memoised on the `cache` object): with a limit below what the trip uses, the trip's days are planned again on a copy of the day records (`packPlanOf(recs, end)`: `planSim` from the shared start with `dayRecs` swapped, like `planWith` in today.js)
- `packComplete`: a day that needs no jacket (`needsJacket`: taken off, the dress code, casual) is complete without one (the code review CR-3-12: each such day cost -1000, so the outfit scores didn't count)
- `packLightRecs(days, AJ, AS)`: each live day rules out (`rec.skip.top` / `.shoes`) every jacket of its kind and every pair of shoes not in the allowed sets, drops its lock, and takes the other kind of jacket (`rec.kind`) when none of its own kind is allowed and the day has no occasion
- pieces picked by hand (`rec.force`) and days already worn are never changed and come first in the allowed sets (`packTally`)
- up to `PACK_TRIES` (3) sets of jackets × 3 sets of shoes are tried, the most worn first (`packSets`), with one suit and one blazer first when the limit is 2+ and the trip has both; measured 100 ms on the test wardrobe
- the best set by `packRate` wins: outfit scores, −50 for a hard check, −1000 for an incomplete outfit, −3000 for each piece over the limit
- the page shows the lighter plan's outfits and list with what it saves (`packSaved`) and the average score before and after; nothing is saved until "Keep this plan" (`packLock` → `packLockDays`, which also saves `rec.kind` for a day that took the other kind)
- `packLockDays` gives every live day of the trip `rec.lock = lockIds(o)` (the app's own lock only covers 7 days); `packLockState` counts the days whose lock is what the plan shows; the page says Locked / n of m days locked / Not locked; `packRelease` removes the locks
- a kept trip's days carry `rec.trip` (`packLockDays`): `clearLocks()` (a planner setting, an occasion) and new planning rules (`replanFuture(true)`) leave them locked, so the packed suitcase stays right; "Start the plan over" (`replanFuture()`) and "Let the plan change again" (`packRelease`) release them (the lock was silently dropped by any Settings change, audit 2026-11-14)
- a piece that won't be here by the trip's first day (on the way or away being cleaned, `!inRotation(i, first day)`: `packLate`) is left out of the trip's plan (`packLateRecs`: `rec.skip` on a copy of the records, used by Pack lighter too) and the Outfits card says so (`#pk-late`)
- `packText(t, days, list)` is the plain-text list (title, count line, groups, travel wear, the outfits per day); `packCopy` uses `navigator.clipboard`, falling back to a hidden textarea; Share (`packShare`) only where `navigator.share` exists
- `tests/pack.test.js`; `PACK_SHOTS=<folder>` saves pictures of the page at 320/390/626px in both themes
- its controls use data-act (act.js; C2-8, 2026-11-22), registered as pack.js's last statement: `packBackTap` (‹), `packDatePick` (`data-which` a | b), `packCapPick` (`data-which` j | s), `packKeepTap` (Keep this plan, Keep these outfits), `packNoCapTap`, `packTickPick` and `packWearTap` (`data-ix`: the line's place in `packLines`; `data-on` 1 = Wear, 0 = Pack), `packWearDayTap` (`data-day`), `packCopyTap`, `packShareTap`, `packReleaseTap`

### `wardrobe.js`

- search still finds the British words: "grey" matches the gray pieces and "trousers" the pants (`wardHay` appends them)
- search in Arabic (fix round 3, W2-16-07): `wardNorm` drops every combining mark (hamza and short vowels too: ابيض finds أبيض), reads ة as ه and ى as ي, drops tatweel and reads Arabic-Indic and Persian digits as 0-9 (2 finds ٢); `tests/ownnames.test.js`
- a watch's Details takes at most `WATCH_DETAILS_MAX` (120) letters (itemsheet.js; W2-12-04)
- search finds a material by its other words (`matSyn`, from core.js `MAT_ALIAS`; 2026-11-15): "fresco" or "high-twist" finds tropical wool, "lisle" mercerized cotton, "broadcloth" poplin, "nylon" a performance stretch shirt; a stored name off the list also finds by the listed name it means ("merino wool" finds socks typed "Merino"); a narrower word ("knitted silk" for silk) is left out
- the one-time rename (`matMigrate`, `MAT_RENAME`, 2026-11-15, the owner's decision on the materials audit): a shirt whose material is exactly "nylon/spandex" or "nylon" (any case: his 10 stretch shirts) becomes "performance stretch", read from the stored wardrobe, saved with a new stamp (`putRaw`, not `put`: a sample piece doesn't become his) and synced (`scheduleSync`); run after the first pull (`whenPulled`) and when a sync brings such a shirt in (dress.js `dressSynced`); idempotent; in the app rather than written to the server because it is his own wording (the wardrobe-edit rule: through the normal sync path)
- the Wardrobe tab's search and sort, the item fields `name` / `brand` / `material` / `notes`, and Delete with Undo (loaded after stats.js; called from `renderWardrobe`, `itemBody` and `rm` (itemsheet.js), `wardList` (item-view.js) and `save` in add.js)
- a grid box's accessible name ends with its tag (`giTag`/`giNote`: Sample, Out of rotation, On the way, at the cleaner's, due for cleaning): the `aria-label` hid the visible tag (re-audit 2026-11-15)
- search: a field above the type chips (`wardBar`, `#wsearch`, `wardQ`); every word typed must be in the item's name, brand, notes, material, type, colour names or pattern (`wardHay`, `wardMatch`; case and accents ignored); it combines with the type chip and stays when the chip changes
- typing redraws only the grid and the count (`wardSearch` → `wardRefresh`: `#wslide`, `#wcount`, `#wclear`), never the field, so the focus and the keyboard stay; a full `renderWardrobe` while the field is in use (a sync arriving) puts the focus and the caret back (`wardKeep`/`wardBack`)
- the (screen-reader) count reads "n items", or "n of m items" when a chip or the search narrows the list (`wardCount`); × in the field clears it (`wardClear`, also Escape)
- nothing matches: `#wnone` (`wardGrid`) says so, with "Clear search" and, when a chip is on and other types match, how many and "Search all types"
- sort: the dropdown beside the search field (`#wsort`, `WARD_SORTS`, `setWardSort`), remembered in `localStorage.ward_sort`: By type, Newest first, Most worn, Longest since worn (never worn first), Colour, A to Z (by the title shown)
- By type follows the type chips' order (`Object.keys(TYPES)`, suits first), newest first within a type
- Most worn and Longest since worn count the days really worn (`wardWear` over `wornDays()` in stats.js) and each box says "Worn n times" / "Last worn 3 Nov" / "Not worn yet" (`.wwear`)
- Colour groups by family (`WARD_FAMS`: neutrals, blues, browns, greens, reds, purples, then pieces shown by their metal; `WARD_FAM` over the planner's `fam`), light to dark within each (`lumCol`, the item's `shade` counted)
- `wardView()` is the list on screen (chip, search, sort) and `wardList()` (item-view.js) returns it, so the item sheet's ‹ ›, swipes and "n of m" follow the filtered, sorted list; it is kept in `wardMemo` until the next redraw (`wardFresh`), since the sheet's swipe asks for it on every touch move
- `wardGrid` asks `wardPic` once a box (each call scheduled the picture's download), and `giTag`/`giNote` are declared outside it (CR-1-13)
- `wardGrid(list)` draws the grid: a box's title is `itemTitle(i)`
- fields: `item.name` (60 characters), `item.brand` (40), `item.material` (40), `item.notes` (500) (`WARD_FIELDS`, `WARD_MAX`); an empty one is removed from the item
- the item sheet's Details card has Name and Brand at its top (`wardFieldsTop`), Material and Notes at its end (`wardFieldsEnd`)
- `setItemField` saves a text field when it is left and 600 ms after the last keystroke (`itemFieldSoon`: a sheet swiped away never tells a field it was left) without redrawing the sheet (the next field tapped would lose its focus); only the header's title and the grid follow
- Material (`matRow`) is None, the type's `MATERIALS_BY` list and the person's own for that type (`matsFor`, 2026-11-16), Other; Other shows "Which material?" and the text typed is the material (`'other'` until something is typed); types without a list show no Material row
- on an Add card, a material photo tagging filled in (tagging.js `takeMaterial`, `q.matAI`) is said under the field ("Material suggested by Claude (medium confidence): …. Change it if it’s wrong.", `.matai`) while the field still holds it; choosing one by hand (`setQField`) drops the mark and sets `q.matSet`, so a later reading leaves it
- the Add card's name, brand and notes are in its More section (checklist.js), saved as typed through `setQField` (an id or a place); `wardSaveFields(q,it)` in `save()` copies them onto the item
- a name is the item's title in the Wardrobe grid, the item sheet's header, Today's pieces list and the piece picker (and their `aria-label`s)
- the fields are ordinary item properties, so they sync, back up, restore and roll back with the item (items merge field by field: core.js mergeItem)
- Delete (`rm` → `wardDelete`) doesn't ask: it removes the item at once, closes the sheet and shows the toast "Deleted <name>" with Undo for `WARD_UNDO_MS` (8 s)
- Undo (`wardUndelete`) writes the item back as it was (same id, photos, close-up, tags, fields, rotation state, its place in `items`) with `updated` one above the deletion's time, and saves again any day record that referred to it and was changed meanwhile (a day re-planned without it)
- why that survives a sync: the tombstone stays, and in `mergeState` a tombstone only wins over an item whose stamp is not newer; so the restored item wins on every device, also on one that had already taken the deletion; a delete that is not undone propagates as before
- `tests/wardrobe.test.js`: search by each field and with a chip, each sort order, the sheet stepping through the filtered list, the fields (sheet, Add card, two synced devices, backup file restored on an empty device), the name in its four places, materials by type, delete / Undo with and without a sync in between, sizes and no sideways scroll at 320 / 390 / 626px in both themes
- Material (`matRow`, types in `MATERIALS_BY`) sits on the Add card above "More details" and at the end of the item sheet's Details
- a text field's typing is also kept in `localStorage.ward_pending` `{id,k,v,was,at}` at each keystroke and saved at once on `pagehide` / the page hidden (`wardFlush`; 2026-11-15: a name typed then Safari swiped away within 0.6 s was lost on a kill, a hidden page's timers stop); `wardPendingRestore` (from `safetyStart`, after the first pull) puts a copy left by a kill back when that field still holds `was` (another device's change since wins), and drops it otherwise
- Delete and Duplicate ignore a second tap while the first runs (`wardOnce`, `wardBusy`, 400 ms after; `wardDelete` → `wardDeleteNow`, `wardDuplicate` → kit.js `kitDuplicate`, one key for Duplicate since the sheet moves to the copy)
- its controls use data-act (act.js; C2-8, 2026-11-22), registered as wardrobe.js's last statement: `wardSearchType` and `wardSearchKey` (the search field's input and keydown), `wardClearTap` (× and "Clear search"), `wardSortPick`, `wardOpenTap` (a box, `data-id`), `wardAllTap` ("Search all types"), `itemFieldType` / `itemFieldDone` (the item sheet's Name, Brand and Notes: `data-id`, `data-f`), `matItemPick` and `matCardPick` (Material: the dropdown's choice, or the "Which material?" field trimmed, `matPicked`)

### `colpick.js`

- its controls use data-act (act.js; Round C wave C2-3, 2026-11-18: 88 inline handlers, none left), registered as its last statement; each handler is a small top-level function after `rdDoneHtml` that calls the function the inline handler called, so `openColPick`, `colPickSel`, `useColPick`, `rdUse` … keep their arguments for the tests and the other files
- the picker: `colBtnTap` (the Color and Pattern buttons: `data-src`, `data-ref`, `data-k`), `cpTileTap` (a tile, its `data-c`), `cpViewTap` (`data-v`), `cpUseTap`, `cpCloseTap`, `cpAskTap`, `cpAskAllTap`, `cpPicsTap`
- New color / New pattern: `cnOpenTap`, `pnOpenTap`, `cnBackTap`, `cnAskTap`, `cnDescType` (`data-act-input`) and `cnDescKey` (`data-act-keydown`: Enter asks) on the words, `cnCloseupTap`, `cnKindTap`, `cnAddTap`, `cnUseOldTap`, `pnAnywayTap`, `cnBasePick` and `cnForPick` (`data-act-change`), `cnTypeTap` (`data-t`), `cnAccentTap`, `cnHexType` / `cnHexDone` (the hex field's input and change), `cnNameType`
- the square and the hue bar: `cnSqPress` / `cnHuePress` (`data-act-pointerdown`; the slider is the handler's `el`, not `currentTarget`, which is `document` now), `cnSqKey` / `cnHueKey` (`data-act-keydown`), `cnSlideUp` (`data-act-keyup`)
- the reading: `rdReadTap` (`data-src`, `data-id`), `rdKeepTap` / `rdChangeTap` / `rdUndoTap` (`data-h`: c, p, m, a), `rdUseTap` (`data-h`, `data-how`), `rdBothTap`, `rdCloseTap`, `rdAgainTap`, `rdUndoAllTap`
- none needs `actsFirst`: no listener added with `addEventListener` sits between these controls and `document` for click, change, input or the keys; `sheetSwipe`'s pointerdown listener on the sheet now runs before the sliders' and leaves them alone (`skip` has `[role=slider]`)
- `tests/colpickacts.test.js` taps every kind of control in Chromium and WebKit and names no `data-act`, so it passes on the code from before too
- every redraw of `#col-sheet` is under `keepFocus` (2026-11-22): `renderColPick(keep)` is `keepFocus($('#col-sheet'), () => colPickDraw(keep))`, and `colPickDraw` chooses the drawer (`renderColNew`, `renderPatNew`, `renderPatPick`, look.js `renderLookPick`, or the color list); a keyboard press on a tile, one of the owner's own colors, a Shade step, an "Offer it for" chip, the views switch or the stripes switch keeps the focus on that control (it fell to `<body>`)
- not while a text field of the sheet has the focus (`cpTyping`: the words, the name, the hex, the Look beta's fields): that redraw runs without `keepFocus` and the focus falls to `<body>` as it always did, so a shade dragged with the Name field focused, or the white-paper check finishing, never brings a phone's keyboard back up (the lead's decision, 2026-11-22)
- a color or pattern tile in its family list carries `data-k="fam"` (`tile(c, extra, k)`): one of the owner's own is listed twice (under "Your colors" / "Your patterns" and in its family) with the same words, and `keepFocus` tells the two copies apart by it
- the sheet's dropdowns ("Behaves like", "For") follow `focusSafe`: after a touch no dropdown is focused by code (the focus goes to its label), after a key press the new dropdown keeps it; `tests/colpickfocus.test.js` (Chromium and WebKit)
- the colour picker (loaded after wardrobe.js): the Colour, Second colour / Toe cap and Third colour fields of the Add card and the item sheet are a button with a chip and the name (`colBtn`), not a `<select>`: the owner couldn't tell what the colour names looked like
- a tap opens `#col-sheet` (z-index 1201, above the item sheet and the pickers): the item's close-up (or photo; a Close-up / Whole photo switch when it has both) edge to edge with a large chip of the colour being looked at, kept at the top while the chips scroll
- under it: "Closest to your close-up/photo" (main colour only, from `item.meas` via `nearestCols`: Very close ≤4 ΔE, Close ≤8, Nearest), then every colour `COLOR_OPTS` allows for the type, grouped by family from Lab (`colFamily`: chroma under 9 neutral, then hue bands) and light to dark; accents start with None
- "Closest to your close-up" uses the measured parts (`item.meas.parts`) for a patterned cloth, and offers nothing for one without them
- an item without a photo shows its drawing in the sheet, redrawn in the colour being looked at (`renderColPick`, the owner's ask); a New chip (Claude's own colour) keeps the item's colour there
- a tap on a chip only previews it; the bottom button "Use <colour>" / "Keep <colour>" saves through `setq` (Add card) or `sete` (editor); Escape closes it without leaving the item (window keydown in the capture phase)
- the sheet keeps an Add card by its id (`colPick.ref`; `openColPick('q', <place>)` from tagging.js and tests is turned into the id), so "Use" changes the card it was opened on after the list moved (CR-1-02: a "Several items" answer landing while it was up made "Use" color another card); a card taken off Add closes its sheet (`renderAdd`, `useColPick`); `colBtn`, `patBtn`, checklist.js `accRow` and `cmpMatchHtml` quote the ref for both sources; `tests/cardid.test.js`
- with no colors (patterns) of one's own, "Your colors" ("Your patterns") is one slim line, the heading and a small dashed "+ New color" button (`.cpownh`, `.cnslim`, `.cpnewl`, still `#cn-open` / `#pn-open` in `.cnown`), so the colors to pick start above the Keep button at every text size; the editor's order stays (Ask Claude, the help line, Your colors, the list) (fix round 3, W2-14-02: at the largest text only "+ New color" showed above the Keep button); the pattern sheet the same way
- for W2-14-02 too: the Ask Claude hint without a close-up (nothing to tap) is one quiet paragraph ("Not sure? Add a close-up and Claude can read the colors.", `.cpaskhint`), and the sheet's photo and swatch are 7 px shorter for each px of text over 16 (`#col-sheet .cpcmp figure`, at least 84 px): at 150% text the first color row starts above the Keep button
- the color and pattern buttons are named "Color: Mid gray", "Pattern: Herringbone" (`colBtn(src,ref,k,v,lab)`; inside their `<label>` the value went unsaid, W2-19-04); the current tile's "Now" is `aria-hidden` with ", now" read after the name
- opening the sheet makes the item sheet behind it inert (`layerInert`), and closing gives the focus back to the button that opened it, or the same field's `.colbtn[data-k]` when it was drawn again (W2-19-03)
- the pattern field is the same kind of button (`patBtn`, `EP` in the editor; a small drawn swatch, `swatchSVG(patVar(…),true)`) and opens the same sheet with k 'pattern' (`renderPatPick`): the close-up beside a large drawing of the item in the pattern looked at (`itemThumb(patVar(it,p),true)`: another pattern is drawn without the item's reading), then every pattern `PATS_BY` allows, drawn, grouped (`PAT_GROUPS`: plain, stripes, checks, weaves, figures, other)
- without a photo the pattern sheet's left side is the item's shaped drawing (`itemThumb(patVar(it,drawAs(sel)))`: a tie, a jacket's panel) in the pattern being looked at, beside the large swatch (the owner's ask)
- Ask Claude (`colAskRun`, `colAskHtml`): with a close-up, the sheet offers to have Claude read the colours again (cost from `benchEstimate(1)`); the request is the tagger's (`ask`) with the white-balanced close-up, the sharp photo (`tagImgOf`, else the photo) and the close-up's measurement, like `refineFromCloseup`
- without a close-up it says to add one first; not connected, the button is off with `NEED_CONN`
- the answer is kept in memory per item (`colAsk`, not saved or synced), so every colour field of that item shows it without asking again; Claude's pick for the field is selected beside the close-up, its runner-up (`altColor`) shown next to it with the confidence and any `newColor`; nothing is saved until "Use …" or "Use all …"
- any colour: the picker's ask sends `freeColors:true` (`tagExtras`); the worker then adds `ownColors {primary, secondary, tertiary: {name, hex} | null}` to the schema and prompt (`FREE_EXTRA`, `OWN_COLORS`, tidied in `cleanTag`; worker/tag.js), Claude's own names and shades not limited to the list; the app keeps them as `ownColors` (`ownColorsOf` in `normTag`, app.js); a normal tag doesn't ask for them
- Ask Claude from the pattern sheet sends `freePattern:true`: the worker adds `ownPattern {name, description, fits}` (`OWN_PATTERN`, `FREE_PAT_EXTRA`, tidied in `cleanTag`; worker/tag.js), Claude's own precise name for the pattern and whether the listed one names it fully
- one own reading per request: a colour sheet sends `freeColors`, the pattern sheet `freePattern`, never both (answers kept apart, `colAsk['<id>|c']` / `colAsk['<id>|p']`); with both the schema is more than the API compiles ("compiled grammar is too large", found on the live worker 2026-11-13), and worker.js drops `freeColors` when both come (a phone with the older app)
- a pattern Claude named that the list lacks (`patNewFor`: the listed one doesn't name it fully and no pattern of that name exists) is a "New · Claude's name" chip drawn like Claude's listed pick, beside "Closest you have" and Claude's second choice
- "Add <name> to your patterns and use it" (`patAddNew`): `addCustomPattern` (synced) with Claude's description, drawn and planned like Claude's listed pick (`patNewBase`), for suits, blazers and trousers together or the item's own type; then set like any pattern; `tests/colpick.test.js`, `tests/tagger.test.js`
- a colour Claude named that the app lacks (`colNewFor`: not a built-in's name, not the owner's own key, not within ΔE00 2 of the listed pick) is offered first as a "New · Claude's exact" chip in Claude's shade (`NEW_COL` '+new'), with "Closest you have" beside it; the listed pick stays selected, so a colour is only added when the owner picks it
- "Add <name> to your colours and use it" (`colAddNew`): `addCustomColor` (synced, `settings.custom`) for the item's type group (`groupOf`), behaving like the nearest built-in colour (`colBaseFor`, as `openColForm`), an accent colour too when it was a second or third colour; then set like any colour
- "Use both colours" / "Use all N colours" (`colAskUseAll`, `.cpall`): shown whenever Claude named more than one colour and any field, the open one included, would change (`colAllDiff`); it takes every colour Claude named, its new ones too (`colAllOf`: each added through `colAddNew`; one already added is the owner's colour of that name), on the Add card and in the editor; the owner wanted to accept all of Claude's colours at once
- `tests/colpick.test.js`
- which colour fields show (`colShows`, `colRows`, `colFieldLabel`; 2026-11-15, the new-user audit): the main colour always but on a watch on a metal bracelet (on a leather strap "Strap colour"); a second colour for patterned cloth, socks ("Toe cap") and sneakers ("Sole"); a third only for patterned cloth; in the item sheet a field already holding a colour stays so it can be cleared; the Shade row hides with the main colour
- `colTidy(it)` drops a second or third colour the type can't show, at Confirm (`saveCard`) and on a type change (`typeTidy`, itemsheet.js)
- "+ A colour of my own" (`colOwnHtml`, `colOwnOpen`, `colOwnSave`) was replaced by the "+ New colour" sheet (2026-11-15)
- the New colour sheet's drawn item follows the finger on the square and the hue bar and a valid hex as typed (`cnFigLive`, called from `cnLive`; the owner's ask, 2026-11-15): only `.cpph.cpfig .cpdraw` is redrawn (`cnDrawn`), at most once a frame (requestAnimationFrame, skipped when the shade is unchanged), about 3-5 ms at 4× CPU throttling and 1-2 ms in WebKit; the whole sheet is still drawn on lift; `tests/colnew.test.js`
- "+ New colour" / "+ New pattern or texture" (2026-11-15, the owner's approved design in `C:\code\audit-2026-11-13\custom-colour-texture\`): states of `#col-sheet` (`colPick.newc` = {kind, from 'pick'|'add'|'settings', hex, h/s/v, name, desc, state, res, base, types, accent, look, closeOn}), `openColNew`/`renderColNew`, `openPatNew`/`renderPatNew`, ‹ back to the list (`colNewBack`; Escape goes back first, then closes)
- the pickers start with "Your colours" / "Your patterns" (`.cnown`): the owner's own for this item, then the dashed "+ New" tile (`#cn-open`, `#pn-open`); own colours and patterns also stay in their family / pattern group (the owner's choice: both places)
- "Describe it to Claude" (`describeAsk` → worker `POST /describe`): the words (≤120, no `<>`), the item's kind and colours, `settings.custom`, and on the pattern sheet the close-up when "Also show Claude my close-up" is ticked (`#pn-close`); busy, result, error (`#cn-err`), another kind (`#cn-kind`: "That sounds like a pattern" → Open patterns, `cnSwitch`) and limit (`.cnlim`) states
- the reuse thresholds (the owner's choice): ΔE00 ≤ 3 "That's your X", "Use X" first (`state 'dup'`); 3-6 Claude's choice first, the other second; over 6 a new colour is the default; a pattern Claude says is already listed ("existing" and in the item's choices) is `state 'reuse'`, "Use X" first
- "Pick the shade": a square (saturation across, brightness down) and a hue bar with pointer capture, arrow keys by 1, and a hex field, kept in step (`cnSetHsv`, `cnLive` while a finger moves, a redraw when it lifts; `cnSlideUp` keeps the slider's focus)
- the cost line: the owner "About N¢" (`describeEstimate`, tagging.js), a tester "N of M lookups left today" from `/me` (`cnLeft`; no cents for testers); none left or not connected: Ask is off
- "Add … and use it" (`colNewAdd`): `addCustomColor({name, hex, base, types, accent})` / `addCustomPattern({…, look})`, then `cnDone` sets the field as `useColPick` does (on the Add card it clears the card's `newColor`/`newPattern`); "Use X instead" is `cnUseExisting`
- the item is drawn in a shade or pattern not yet added through a stand-in key removed again (`cnDrawn`: `COLORS.cnprev`; `cnWithPat`: `CUSTOM_PAT['own-cnprev']` with the look)
- from Settings (`openCustomNew(kind)`): `colPick.src 's'` with a sample item (`colPick.fake`, `CN_SAMPLE`) and a "For" select (`#cn-for`, `cnSetType`); `colTarget()` gives the sample
- testers add colours and patterns too: theirs are in their own `settings.custom`, synced in their namespace
- `tests/colnew.test.js` (/describe mocked by `page.route`)
- white paper in the close-up on the sheets (2026-11-15, the owner's choice from `C:\code\audit-2026-11-13\custom-colour-texture\photo-wb-mockups\`, screens 07-10): the colour sheet has "Also show Claude my close-up" too (`#cn-close`; the pattern sheet's is `#pn-close`) whenever the item has a close-up (`cnHasClose`); the colour box starts ticked when the close-up has paper and unticked when not (`cnPaperDefault`, until the owner taps it: `closeSet`); the pattern box still starts unticked
- under both boxes the paper line (`cnPaperLine`, `#cn-paper`, `.wbst` in `.cnask`): "White paper found: Claude sees the true color(s)." / "No white paper in this close-up: the color may be off." / the warm wording, with Retake close-up in the item sheet's editor only (`cnRetake` → `replaceTexture`; not on an Add card, whose Replace re-tags)
- the request (`describeAsk`): `closeup` + `wb` from `paperCopy` (tagging.js) when the box is ticked, for colours and patterns; no close-up, no `wb` (the worker reads it with `wbOf`/`lightText`); what was sent is kept as `nc.sent` {wb, warm}
- Claude's colour then carries a note (`cnLightNote`, `#cn-warm`): sent without paper in warm light, "Taken in warm light — …" (`.cnwarm`) with Retake close-up; without paper otherwise, "No white paper in the close-up, so this shade is Claude’s best guess from the photo as taken."
- the sheets' Retake close-up (`cnRetakeHtml`, in the paper line and the warm-light note) opens the close-up chooser on a touch screen (ui.js `closeupBtn`, mode 'retake'); without touch it is the file window
- a name already a colour's (built-in or own, by key or as shown, letters only: "Navy", "light blue", "Very dark navy") is refused (`colNameTaken`, checked in `addCustomColor`)
- no Ask Claude block for a type without a close-up (shoes, belts, watches, socks, coats, clips, links: it said to add a fabric close-up that can't be added)
- `tests/itemsheet.test.js`
- a grey spelled another way ("Gray", "Dark gray", "Light gray", "Mid gray": a name whose letters with gray→grey are a colour key or a grey in `COL_ALIAS`) is taken too (`colNameTaken`, 2026-11-15, the re-audit); other aliases ("Cognac", "Teal") stay free for an own shade
- own colour and pattern names are text, never HTML (2026-11-15, the re-audit's N4: `<img src=x onerror=…>` as a name ran in the colour button, the picker's tiles and the pattern button, and synced): the colour button, tiles, big swatch's caption, the Use/Keep button and the pattern button escape it (`esc`), on top of the names being cleaned (core.js `ownNameClean`); `tests/ownnames.test.js`
- "Read color and texture from close-up" (2026-11-15, the owner's variant A from `C:\code\audit-2026-11-13\custom-colour-texture\closeup-reading-mockups\` with his changes; `rd*`): one tap sends `POST /describe {what:'both'}` with the close-up and `wb` from `paperCopy` and no words (`rdRun`), and shows `#rd-sheet` (z-index 1206, over the color sheet, under the close-up chooser; in ui.js `LAYER_Q`): the close-up, the item drawn with both (`rdDraw`/`rdPreview`, stand-in keys as `cnDrawn`), the paper line, then a color card and a pattern & texture card (`rdColCard`, `rdPatCard`) each with Keep / Use, and "Use both" pinned at the bottom (`rdBar`, `rdBoth`)
- its rules are the New color / New pattern sheets': ΔE00 ≤ 3 to a color the item may have puts "That's your X" / Use X first with "Add <Claude's> as a new color instead" (`dup`); over 3 Claude's first, with "Use X instead" up to ΔE 6; a pattern Claude says is listed is "That's X" (`reuse`); a new one is added with `addCustomColor` / `addCustomPattern` (`rdAddCol`, `rdAddPat`; an own one of that name is reused, a taken name gets " tone" / " weave") and set through `setq` / `sete` (`rdSet`)
- nothing changes until a tap; Use on one card sets that half and the sheet stays for the other; when both cards are decided the sheet closes with a toast ("Color and texture updated." / "Color updated." / "Texture updated.") with Undo, and a card under the button (`rdDoneHtml`, `#rd-done`) shows the new drawing, what changed and Undo (`rdUndo`/`rdUndoAll`: the fields as they were, `RD_COLF`/`RD_PATF`, and a color or pattern the reading added removed when nothing else uses it)
- the warm-light / no-paper note on the color card (`rdLightNote`) and "That doesn't look like a close-up of cloth" carry Retake close-up (`rdRetake`: replaces the close-up, then reads again); a half that failed says "Claude couldn't read the color/weave this time" and the other stays usable
- cost under the buttons (`rdCost`): the owner "About 4¢" (`readEstimate`), a tester "2 lookups · N of 40 left today"; with no connection, or a tester with fewer than 2 left, the button is grayed (`aria-disabled`) and a tap says why (`rdOff`); a reading adds the worker's `lookups` to `acct.me.used.describe`
- `tests/closeupread.test.js` (Chromium and WebKit, touch, /describe stubbed)
- swiping down closes the color / pattern sheet (its New color / New pattern states too) and the reading sheet (2026-11-15, the owner: "When I click on color or texture, the sheet comes up, but I can't swipe it down"): add.js `sheetSwipe` through `cpSwipe(sheet, head, backdrop, on, close)` (`slide`, `fadeBack`), installed once from `openColPick`/`openCustomNew` (`colSwipe`) and `rdEls`; the sticky top (`.cptop` with its grabber `.cpgrab` / `SW_GRAB`, `.rdtop`) is a handle at any time, the rest only at `scrollTop` 0; closes past a quarter or on a flick as × does (then slides away, `.cpgoing`), else springs back; never from fields, dropdowns, `[role=slider]` (the shade square, the hue bar) or the bottom bars; `tests/editorrework.test.js`
- the color / pattern sheet and the reading sheet are at most `min(92vh, 100vh - var(--ivtop))` tall, so their top is below the status bar (stand-in `--sat` 59px)
- Ask Claude comes right under the Close-up / Whole photo / Drawing switch in both pickers (2026-11-15, the owner's ask), then the help line, then Your colors / Your patterns, then the rest
- the material card (2026-11-15, the materials audit; `rdMatOf`, `rdMatCard`, `me.m`): the worker's pattern half gives `material {key, confidence, why}` (no third call, still 2 lookups); a third card shows for high or medium on one of the type's materials (or the person's own) the item doesn't already have: "Material: Flannel", Keep blank / Use Flannel and Claude's line; when the item has another material, "Yours: Wool · Claude: Flannel" (Keep Wool / Use Flannel). Since 2026-11-16: a low one is a guess card ("Claude’s guess: cotton", Keep / Use cotton, `.rdmatguess`); a new one (`new:true`, medium or high) is "Claude calls it cupro" with Keep / "Add cupro as a material" (`.rdmatnew`: `addOwnMaterial`, then set; Undo takes it off and out of the list when nothing else uses it). "Use all" / the bar never applies a guess or adds a material (`rdMatMain` null); the reading sends `ownMaterials` for the item's type
- the bar is "Use all" with three cards to decide ("Use both" with two); Use all never changes a material already set (`rdMatMain` null: the card is kept), only the card's own Use does; Use sets `item.material` (`rdSetMat`: `setItemField`, on an Add card `setQField`), Undo puts the old one back (`rdLast.m`), the toast names what changed ("Color, texture and material updated."); `tests/materials.test.js`
- a colour sheet's `colShows` shows the main colour on a watch with any strap (`strapBand`: leather, rubber, NATO; 2026-11-15)
- the reading sheet closes with ×, Stop/Close, the backdrop, Escape and the phone's Back (`rdHist`, back.js kind 'rd'); the colour sheet's `backOver` stands aside while it holds the entry; `tests/backgesture.test.js`
- New color / New pattern (and Settings' New color): no field redraws the sheet on `change` (picker audit 2026-11-17 #1: the Name field's blur redrew it between the finger going down and the click, so the first tap on "Add … and use it" was lost); `cnName` updates the button, the caption, "Add … anyway" and shows the hidden `#cn-how` in place; the hex field's change updates in place too (`cnLive`, `cnBaseLive`)
- the pattern sheet's "Not sure? Add a close-up" hint shows only for close-up types (`TEXTURE_TYPES`), as the color sheet's (picker audit #2)
- that hint (`cpAskHint`) says "Add a close-up in Pictures, at the bottom" in the item sheet, where "Pictures, at the bottom" is a link (`cpGoPics`: closes the picker, opens Pictures, scrolls to its Close-up button); on the Add card it names no place (picker audit #3)
- `tests/pickbugs.test.js` covers the picker audit's four bugs with real taps and clicks, Chromium and WebKit

### `checklist.js`

- the checklist redesign of the item sheet and the Add card (2026-11-16, the owner's approved mockup `C:\code\audit-2026-11-13\item-redesign\prototype\compare-side.html`, direction "2B Checklist" of `DESIGN.md`; `INVENTORY.md` lists what both screens keep); loaded after colpick.js, nothing runs at load
- a section (`ckSec(scope, k, {mark, title, sum, body, open, id, cls})`, `.cks` / `.ckh` / `.ckb`): a status mark (`ckMark`: `good` ✓, `warn` !, `opt` / `todo` ○, `ai` ✦; `role=img` with "Done" / "Worth a look" / "Optional" / "Not checked yet" / "Claude suggests"), a title and a one-line summary in a 62px header button (`aria-expanded`), the body folded with CSS (`display:none`, still in the DOM)
- which sections are open is kept in memory (`CK_OPEN`, `ckIsOpen`, `ckToggle`): the item sheet shares one set (scope `e`), so a section opened on one item stays open on the next; each Add card has its own (`q|<id>`)
- the comparison (`cmpHtml(key, closeUp, drawnSvg, paper)`, `.cmpw` / `.cmpv` / `.cmplv`): the close-up left ("Close-up" top left, "White paper" badge bottom left when paper was found), the drawing right ("Drawn" top right, the zoom `.cmpz` bottom right), both the same size and crop; the drawing is the item's own fabric SVG (`cmpDrawn`: `itemThumb(it,true)` without the close-up and swatch, ids made stable, drawn at `CMP_DW` 640)
- a tap (a `click`, so Enter and Space too) on either square slides them together (`cmpOv`, `.ovl`, `--d` = (half a square + the gap) / 2 each, 0.38 s, none with reduced motion; `.cmpnow` stops the slide on a redraw or resize): the same size, overlapping by half, the close-up on top (z-index 3), its inner corners square, no shadow; a second tap slides them apart; state per item / card in `CMP` ({ov, z, cu, cv, op}) while the app runs, so a redraw keeps it
- in the overlap both show the same spot: the close-up draws the left two-thirds of the region shown and the drawing the right two-thirds, each as a square (no stretching: the region is two-thirds as tall too, centered); the close-up's right half is cut with `destination-out` (solid at the seam, `op` across, gone at its right edge) so the drawing shows through (`cmpPaint`)
- the slider "Close-up ◀ ▶ Drawn" (`.cmpop`, 0–100, default 50) shows only while overlapped and sets `op`; − / + (`.cmpzo` / `.cmpzi`, ×1.5, 1–4×), a two-finger pinch over either square (Pointer Events, both together), Ctrl+wheel (a trackpad's pinch; a plain wheel scrolls), +/− keys; a drag pans when zoomed (`touch-action` none then, else pan-y); a drag or pinch doesn't toggle (`data-moved`); no lens, no hint text
- the pictures' touchstart doesn't reach the sheets (stopPropagation): the item sheet's swipe down and sideways stepping, and the no-photo sheet's swipe, don't start on them
- pictures are drawn straight from Image elements (no copies), kept for `CMP_KEEP` (4) items / cards (`CMP_PIC`); listeners and a ResizeObserver are put on after each redraw (`cmpMount`, from `renderItem`, `renderAdd`, `npsDraw`, and `ckToggle` when a section opens)
- under it (`cmpMatchHtml`): the Darker · Same · Lighter bar (`.cmpbar`), "Drawn N steps lighter/darker than the cloth" or "The drawing matches the cloth" (`cmpDiff`: the shade minus `shadeFor` the measured Lab), Match close-up (`cmpMatchGo` → add.js `matchShadeQ` / `matchShadeE`, gold) and Undo (`cmpMatchUndo`, the shade before, `CMP_UNDO`)
- the measured Lab (`cmpLab`): the item's or card's `meas` from the close-up, else measured once from the close-up (`CMP_LAB` by `texSig`) and the sheet drawn again; until then "Measuring the close-up…"
- a close-up that can't be measured (the picture doesn't load, the phone refuses a canvas, no color comes out) is `CMP_LAB[k]=false` and says "Couldn’t measure the close-up." (`.cmpfail`; fix round A, CR-4-13: it said "Measuring…" for good); `tests/cmpmeasure.test.js`
- each comparison's size observer disconnects itself once its drawing has left the page (fix round A, CR-4-14: every redraw made one that watched forever)
- white paper (`cmpPaper`): tagging.js `paperNow` / `paperOf` (asked once per picture pair, `CMP_ASKED`, then drawn again), an Add card's `meas.ref` meanwhile; the line under the comparison (`cmpPaperLine`, `.wbst.cmpwb`) "White paper found · Colors set from it." / "No white paper · …" with Retake close-up; on the item sheet `paperLine` (`#ivwb`, right after a close-up was added) takes its place
- `tests/itemredesign.test.js` (Chromium and WebKit, 390 light and 690 dark)
